## Integrate the <SplashCursor /> component from React Bits

You are helping integrate an open-source React component into an existing application.

### Component: SplashCursor
### Variant: JavaScript + CSS


---

### Usage Example
```jsx
import SplashCursor from './SplashCursor';

<SplashCursor
  rainbow
  size={1}
  force={1}
  swirl={3}
  trail={1}
  clickSplash
/>
```

### Props
| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
|------|------|---------|-------------|
| colors | string[] | ['#4f8cff', '#2dd4bf', '#ffd166'] | Palette the ink cycles through when rainbow is off. |
| rainbow | boolean | true | Cycles through the full spectrum instead of the palette. |
| colorSpeed | number | 1 | How fast the colors shift while you move. 0 keeps one color per stroke. |
| intensity | number | 1 | How much ink each movement leaves behind. |
| size | number | 1 | Width of the stroke and the splash. |
| force | number | 1 | How hard the cursor pushes the fluid. |
| swirl | number | 3 | Amount of curling eddies in the flow. |
| trail | number | 1 | How long the ink lingers before it fades. 2 lasts twice as long. |
| momentum | number | 1 | How long the fluid keeps moving after the cursor stops. |
| shading | number | 1 | Depth shading on the ripples, from 0 to 1. |
| glow | number | 0 | Soft bloom around the brightest ink, from 0 to 1. |
| quality | 'low' | 'medium' | 'high' | 'high' | Simulation detail. Lower settings are lighter on older devices. |
| clickSplash | boolean | true | Bursts a splash where you click or tap. |
| intro | boolean | true | Plays a short stroke across the screen on mount. |
| contained | boolean | false | Fills the parent element instead of the whole viewport and only reacts inside it. |
| theme | 'dark' | 'light' | 'dark' | Glowing ink for dark backgrounds or absorbing ink for light ones. |
| zIndex | number | 50 | Stacking order of the effect over your content. |
| className | string | '' | Extra class names for the container. |
| style | CSSProperties | - | Inline styles for the container. |

### Full Component Source
```jsx
'use client';

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import './SplashCursor.css';

const QUALITY = {
  low: { sim: 64, dye: 512, iterations: 10 },
  medium: { sim: 96, dye: 1024, iterations: 16 },
  high: { sim: 128, dye: 1440, iterations: 20 }
};
const BLOOM_RESOLUTION = 256;
const BLOOM_ITERATIONS = 4;
const BLOOM_THRESHOLD = 0.3;
const BLOOM_KNEE = 0.7;

const ROOT = 'splash-cursor';
const CANVAS = 'splash-cursor__canvas';

const VERTEX = `#version 300 es
precision highp float;
in vec2 aPosition;
uniform vec2 texelSize;
out vec2 vUv;
out vec2 vL;
out vec2 vR;
out vec2 vT;
out vec2 vB;
void main() {
  vUv = aPosition * 0.5 + 0.5;
  vL = vUv - vec2(texelSize.x, 0.0);
  vR = vUv + vec2(texelSize.x, 0.0);
  vT = vUv + vec2(0.0, texelSize.y);
  vB = vUv - vec2(0.0, texelSize.y);
  gl_Position = vec4(aPosition, 0.0, 1.0);
}`;

const HEADER = `#version 300 es
precision highp float;
precision highp sampler2D;
in vec2 vUv;
in vec2 vL;
in vec2 vR;
in vec2 vT;
in vec2 vB;
out vec4 outColor;
`;

const SHADERS = {
  copy: `uniform sampler2D uTexture;
void main() {
  outColor = texture(uTexture, vUv);
}`,
  clear: `uniform sampler2D uTexture;
uniform float value;
void main() {
  outColor = value * texture(uTexture, vUv);
}`,
  splat: `uniform sampler2D uTarget;
uniform float aspectRatio;
uniform vec3 color;
uniform vec2 pointA;
uniform vec2 pointB;
uniform float radius;
void main() {
  vec2 scale = vec2(aspectRatio, 1.0);
  vec2 pa = (vUv - pointA) * scale;
  vec2 ba = (pointB - pointA) * scale;
  float h = clamp(dot(pa, ba) / max(dot(ba, ba), 1e-9), 0.0, 1.0);
  vec2 d = pa - ba * h;
  vec3 base = texture(uTarget, vUv).xyz;
  outColor = vec4(base + exp(-dot(d, d) / radius) * color, 1.0);
}`,
  burst: `uniform sampler2D uTarget;
uniform float aspectRatio;
uniform vec2 point;
uniform float radius;
uniform float strength;
uniform float seed;
void main() {
  vec2 d = (vUv - point) * vec2(aspectRatio, 1.0);
  float distance = length(d);
  vec2 direction = d / max(distance, 0.00001);
  float angle = atan(d.y, d.x);
  float petals = 0.78 + 0.22 * sin(angle * 5.0 + seed) * sin(angle * 3.0 - seed * 1.7);
  vec2 twist = vec2(-direction.y, direction.x) * 0.3 * sin(angle * 4.0 + seed * 2.3);
  float falloff = exp(-dot(d, d) / radius) * distance / sqrt(radius);
  outColor = vec4(texture(uTarget, vUv).xy + (direction * petals + twist) * falloff * strength, 0.0, 1.0);
}`,
  crown: `uniform sampler2D uTarget;
uniform float aspectRatio;
uniform vec2 point;
uniform float radius;
uniform vec3 colorA;
uniform vec3 colorB;
uniform float seed;
void main() {
  vec2 d = (vUv - point) * vec2(aspectRatio, 1.0);
  vec3 ink = exp(-dot(d, d) / (radius * 0.45)) * colorA;
  for (int i = 0; i < 7; i++) {
    float index = float(i);
    float angle = seed + index * 0.8975979 + sin(seed * 3.0 + index * 1.9) * 0.35;
    float reach = sqrt(radius) * (0.75 + 0.45 * fract(sin(seed * 7.1 + index * 3.7) * 43.7));
    float drop = radius * (0.08 + 0.12 * fract(sin(seed * 2.9 + index * 5.3) * 31.9));
    vec2 q = d - vec2(cos(angle), sin(angle)) * reach;
    ink += exp(-dot(q, q) / drop) * mix(colorA, colorB, fract(index * 0.37 + 0.2));
  }
  outColor = vec4(texture(uTarget, vUv).xyz + ink, 1.0);
}`,
  advection: `uniform sampler2D uVelocity;
uniform sampler2D uSource;
uniform vec2 texelSize;
uniform float dt;
uniform float dissipation;
void main() {
  vec2 coord = vUv - dt * texture(uVelocity, vUv).xy * texelSize;
  outColor = texture(uSource, coord) / (1.0 + dissipation * dt);
}`,
  divergence: `uniform sampler2D uVelocity;
void main() {
  float L = texture(uVelocity, vL).x;
  float R = texture(uVelocity, vR).x;
  float T = texture(uVelocity, vT).y;
  float B = texture(uVelocity, vB).y;
  vec2 C = texture(uVelocity, vUv).xy;
  if (vL.x < 0.0) L = -C.x;
  if (vR.x > 1.0) R = -C.x;
  if (vT.y > 1.0) T = -C.y;
  if (vB.y < 0.0) B = -C.y;
  outColor = vec4(0.5 * (R - L + T - B), 0.0, 0.0, 1.0);
}`,
  curl: `uniform sampler2D uVelocity;
void main() {
  float L = texture(uVelocity, vL).y;
  float R = texture(uVelocity, vR).y;
  float T = texture(uVelocity, vT).x;
  float B = texture(uVelocity, vB).x;
  outColor = vec4(0.5 * (R - L - T + B), 0.0, 0.0, 1.0);
}`,
  vorticity: `uniform sampler2D uVelocity;
uniform sampler2D uCurl;
uniform float curl;
uniform float dt;
void main() {
  float L = texture(uCurl, vL).x;
  float R = texture(uCurl, vR).x;
  float T = texture(uCurl, vT).x;
  float B = texture(uCurl, vB).x;
  float C = texture(uCurl, vUv).x;
  vec2 force = 0.5 * vec2(abs(T) - abs(B), abs(R) - abs(L));
  force /= length(force) + 0.0001;
  force *= curl * C;
  force.y *= -1.0;
  vec2 velocity = texture(uVelocity, vUv).xy + force * dt;
  outColor = vec4(clamp(velocity, -1000.0, 1000.0), 0.0, 1.0);
}`,
  pressure: `uniform sampler2D uPressure;
uniform sampler2D uDivergence;
void main() {
  float L = texture(uPressure, vL).x;
  float R = texture(uPressure, vR).x;
  float T = texture(uPressure, vT).x;
  float B = texture(uPressure, vB).x;
  float divergence = texture(uDivergence, vUv).x;
  outColor = vec4((L + R + B + T - divergence) * 0.25, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0);
}`,
  gradient: `uniform sampler2D uPressure;
uniform sampler2D uVelocity;
void main() {
  float L = texture(uPressure, vL).x;
  float R = texture(uPressure, vR).x;
  float T = texture(uPressure, vT).x;
  float B = texture(uPressure, vB).x;
  vec2 velocity = texture(uVelocity, vUv).xy - vec2(R - L, T - B);
  outColor = vec4(velocity, 0.0, 1.0);
}`,
  prefilter: `uniform sampler2D uTexture;
uniform vec3 curve;
uniform float threshold;
void main() {
  vec3 c = texture(uTexture, vUv).rgb;
  float br = max(c.r, max(c.g, c.b));
  float rq = clamp(br - curve.x, 0.0, curve.y);
  rq = curve.z * rq * rq;
  c *= max(rq, br - threshold) / max(br, 0.0001);
  outColor = vec4(c, 0.0);
}`,
  blur: `uniform sampler2D uTexture;
uniform float intensity;
void main() {
  vec4 sum = texture(uTexture, vL) + texture(uTexture, vR) + texture(uTexture, vT) + texture(uTexture, vB);
  outColor = sum * 0.25 * intensity;
}`,
  display: `uniform sampler2D uTexture;
uniform sampler2D uBloom;
uniform vec2 texelSize;
uniform float shading;
uniform float bloom;
uniform float light;
float hash(vec2 p) {
  vec3 q = fract(vec3(p.xyx) * 0.1031);
  q += dot(q, q.yzx + 33.33);
  return fract((q.x + q.y) * q.z);
}
void main() {
  vec3 c = texture(uTexture, vUv).rgb;
  if (shading > 0.0) {
    float dx = length(texture(uTexture, vR).rgb) - length(texture(uTexture, vL).rgb);
    float dy = length(texture(uTexture, vT).rgb) - length(texture(uTexture, vB).rgb);
    vec3 n = normalize(vec3(dx, dy, length(texelSize)));
    c *= mix(1.0, clamp(n.z + 0.7, 0.7, 1.0), shading);
  }
  if (bloom > 0.0) {
    c += max(texture(uBloom, vUv).rgb, 0.0) * bloom;
  }
  c = max(c, 0.0);
  float peak = max(c.r, max(c.g, c.b));
  float grain = (hash(gl_FragCoord.xy) - 0.5) / 255.0 * smoothstep(0.0, 0.03, peak);
  if (light > 0.5) {
    vec3 transmit = exp(-2.4 * c);
    float low = min(transmit.r, min(transmit.g, transmit.b));
    float alpha = clamp(1.0 - low + grain, 0.0, 1.0);
    outColor = vec4(min(transmit - low, vec3(alpha)), alpha);
  } else {
    c = max(c + grain, 0.0);
    float alpha = clamp(max(c.r, max(c.g, c.b)), 0.0, 1.0);
    outColor = vec4(min(c, vec3(alpha)), alpha);
  }
}`
};

const clamp = (value, min, max) => Math.min(max, Math.max(min, value));

const parseColor = value => {
  let hex = String(value).replace('#', '').trim();
  if (hex.length === 3) hex = [...hex].map(char => char + char).join('');
  const number = parseInt(hex.slice(0, 6), 16);
  if (Number.isNaN(number)) return [1, 1, 1];
  return [((number >> 16) & 255) / 255, ((number >> 8) & 255) / 255, (number & 255) / 255];
};

const hsv = (h, s, v) => {
  const i = Math.floor(h * 6);
  const f = h * 6 - i;
  const p = v * (1 - s);
  const q = v * (1 - f * s);
  const t = v * (1 - (1 - f) * s);
  const sector = ((i % 6) + 6) % 6;
  if (sector === 0) return [v, t, p];
  if (sector === 1) return [q, v, p];
  if (sector === 2) return [p, v, t];
  if (sector === 3) return [p, q, v];
  if (sector === 4) return [t, p, v];
  return [v, p, q];
};

function SplashCursor({
  colors = ['#4f8cff', '#2dd4bf', '#ffd166'],
  rainbow = true,
  colorSpeed = 1,
  intensity = 1,
  size = 1,
  force = 1,
  swirl = 3,
  trail = 1,
  momentum = 1,
  shading = 1,
  glow = 0,
  quality = 'high',
  clickSplash = true,
  intro = true,
  contained = false,
  theme = 'dark',
  zIndex = 50,
  className = '',
  style
}) {
  const rootRef = useRef(null);
  const canvasRef = useRef(null);
  const releaseRef = useRef(0);
  const [generation, setGeneration] = useState(0);

  const settings = {
    palette: (Array.isArray(colors) && colors.length ? colors : ['#ffffff']).map(parseColor),
    rainbow,
    colorSpeed: Math.max(0, colorSpeed),
    intensity: Math.max(0, intensity),
    size: Math.max(0.05, size),
    force: Math.max(0, force),
    swirl: Math.max(0, swirl),
    trail: Math.max(0.05, trail),
    momentum: Math.max(0.05, momentum),
    shading: clamp(shading, 0, 1),
    glow: clamp(glow, 0, 1),
    quality: QUALITY[quality] ? quality : 'high',
    clickSplash,
    contained,
    light: theme === 'light'
  };
  const settingsRef = useRef(settings);
  settingsRef.current = settings;
  const introRef = useRef(intro);

  useEffect(() => {
    const parent = rootRef.current?.parentElement;
    if (!contained || !parent || getComputedStyle(parent).position !== 'static') return undefined;
    const previous = parent.style.position;
    parent.style.position = 'relative';
    return () => {
      parent.style.position = previous;
    };
  }, [contained]);

  useEffect(() => {
    window.clearTimeout(releaseRef.current);
    const canvas = canvasRef.current;
    if (!canvas) return undefined;
    const gl = canvas.getContext('webgl2', {
      alpha: true,
      premultipliedAlpha: true,
      antialias: false,
      depth: false,
      stencil: false,
      preserveDrawingBuffer: false
    });
    if (!gl) return undefined;
    gl.getExtension('EXT_color_buffer_float');
    gl.getExtension('EXT_color_buffer_half_float');
    const parallel = gl.getExtension('KHR_parallel_shader_compile');

    const renderable = (internal, format) => {
      const texture = gl.createTexture();
      gl.bindTexture(gl.TEXTURE_2D, texture);
      gl.texImage2D(gl.TEXTURE_2D, 0, internal, 4, 4, 0, format, gl.HALF_FLOAT, null);
      const fbo = gl.createFramebuffer();
      gl.bindFramebuffer(gl.FRAMEBUFFER, fbo);
      gl.framebufferTexture2D(gl.FRAMEBUFFER, gl.COLOR_ATTACHMENT0, gl.TEXTURE_2D, texture, 0);
      const complete = gl.checkFramebufferStatus(gl.FRAMEBUFFER) === gl.FRAMEBUFFER_COMPLETE;
      gl.bindFramebuffer(gl.FRAMEBUFFER, null);
      gl.deleteFramebuffer(fbo);
      gl.deleteTexture(texture);
      return complete;
    };
    const rgba = { internal: gl.RGBA16F, format: gl.RGBA };
    if (!renderable(rgba.internal, rgba.format)) return undefined;
    const rg = renderable(gl.RG16F, gl.RG) ? { internal: gl.RG16F, format: gl.RG } : rgba;
    const r = renderable(gl.R16F, gl.RED) ? { internal: gl.R16F, format: gl.RED } : rg;

    const compile = (type, source) => {
      const shader = gl.createShader(type);
      gl.shaderSource(shader, source);
      gl.compileShader(shader);
      return shader;
    };
    const vertex = compile(gl.VERTEX_SHADER, VERTEX);
    const programs = {};
    Object.entries(SHADERS).forEach(([name, body]) => {
      const fragment = compile(gl.FRAGMENT_SHADER, HEADER + body);
      const program = gl.createProgram();
      gl.attachShader(program, vertex);
      gl.attachShader(program, fragment);
      gl.bindAttribLocation(program, 0, 'aPosition');
      gl.linkProgram(program);
      gl.deleteShader(fragment);
      programs[name] = { program, uniforms: {} };
    });
    gl.deleteShader(vertex);

    let ready = false;
    let broken = false;

    const complete = () =>
      !parallel ||
      Object.values(programs).every(item => gl.getProgramParameter(item.program, parallel.COMPLETION_STATUS_KHR));

    const finish = () => {
      if (!complete()) return false;
      Object.values(programs).forEach(item => {
        if (!gl.getProgramParameter(item.program, gl.LINK_STATUS)) broken = true;
        const count = gl.getProgramParameter(item.program, gl.ACTIVE_UNIFORMS);
        for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
          const info = gl.getActiveUniform(item.program, i);
          if (info) item.uniforms[info.name] = gl.getUniformLocation(item.program, info.name);
        }
      });
      ready = true;
      return true;
    };

    const buffer = gl.createBuffer();
    const vao = gl.createVertexArray();
    gl.bindVertexArray(vao);
    gl.bindBuffer(gl.ARRAY_BUFFER, buffer);
    gl.bufferData(gl.ARRAY_BUFFER, new Float32Array([-1, -1, 1, -1, -1, 1, 1, 1]), gl.STATIC_DRAW);
    gl.enableVertexAttribArray(0);
    gl.vertexAttribPointer(0, 2, gl.FLOAT, false, 0, 0);

    const targets = new Set();
    let disposed = false;
    let lost = false;

    const retire = () => {
      if (gl.isContextLost()) return;
      if (!ready && !complete()) {
        requestAnimationFrame(retire);
        return;
      }
      Object.values(programs).forEach(item => gl.deleteProgram(item.program));
    };

    const release = () => {
      if (gl.isContextLost()) return;
      targets.forEach(target => {
        gl.deleteFramebuffer(target.fbo);
        gl.deleteTexture(target.texture);
      });
      targets.clear();
      retire();
      gl.deleteBuffer(buffer);
      gl.deleteVertexArray(vao);
    };

    if (!parallel) finish();
    if (broken) {
      release();
      return undefined;
    }

    const use = name => {
      const item = programs[name];
      gl.useProgram(item.program);
      return item.uniforms;
    };

    const blit = target => {
      if (target) {
        gl.viewport(0, 0, target.width, target.height);
        gl.bindFramebuffer(gl.FRAMEBUFFER, target.fbo);
      } else {
        gl.viewport(0, 0, gl.drawingBufferWidth, gl.drawingBufferHeight);
        gl.bindFramebuffer(gl.FRAMEBUFFER, null);
      }
      gl.drawArrays(gl.TRIANGLE_STRIP, 0, 4);
    };

    const createTarget = (width, height, format, filter) => {
      gl.activeTexture(gl.TEXTURE0);
      const texture = gl.createTexture();
      gl.bindTexture(gl.TEXTURE_2D, texture);
      gl.texParameteri(gl.TEXTURE_2D, gl.TEXTURE_MIN_FILTER, filter);
      gl.texParameteri(gl.TEXTURE_2D, gl.TEXTURE_MAG_FILTER, filter);
      gl.texParameteri(gl.TEXTURE_2D, gl.TEXTURE_WRAP_S, gl.CLAMP_TO_EDGE);
      gl.texParameteri(gl.TEXTURE_2D, gl.TEXTURE_WRAP_T, gl.CLAMP_TO_EDGE);
      gl.texImage2D(gl.TEXTURE_2D, 0, format.internal, width, height, 0, format.format, gl.HALF_FLOAT, null);
      const fbo = gl.createFramebuffer();
      gl.bindFramebuffer(gl.FRAMEBUFFER, fbo);
      gl.framebufferTexture2D(gl.FRAMEBUFFER, gl.COLOR_ATTACHMENT0, gl.TEXTURE_2D, texture, 0);
      gl.viewport(0, 0, width, height);
      gl.clearColor(0, 0, 0, 0);
      gl.clear(gl.COLOR_BUFFER_BIT);
      const target = {
        texture,
        fbo,
        width,
        height,
        texelX: 1 / width,
        texelY: 1 / height,
        attach: unit => {
          gl.activeTexture(gl.TEXTURE0 + unit);
          gl.bindTexture(gl.TEXTURE_2D, texture);
          return unit;
        }
      };
      targets.add(target);
      return target;
    };

    const destroyTarget = target => {
      if (!target) return;
      gl.deleteFramebuffer(target.fbo);
      gl.deleteTexture(target.texture);
      targets.delete(target);
    };

    const reshape = (target, width, height, format) => {
      gl.bindTexture(gl.TEXTURE_2D, target.texture);
      gl.texImage2D(gl.TEXTURE_2D, 0, format.internal, width, height, 0, format.format, gl.HALF_FLOAT, null);
      target.width = width;
      target.height = height;
      target.texelX = 1 / width;
      target.texelY = 1 / height;
    };

    const createDouble = (width, height, format, filter) => {
      const pair = {
        read: createTarget(width, height, format, filter),
        write: createTarget(width, height, format, filter),
        width,
        height,
        texelX: 1 / width,
        texelY: 1 / height,
        swap: () => {
          const current = pair.read;
          pair.read = pair.write;
          pair.write = current;
        }
      };
      return pair;
    };

    const resizeDouble = (pair, width, height, format, filter, keep) => {
      if (keep) {
        const next = createTarget(width, height, format, filter);
        const u = use('copy');
        gl.uniform1i(u.uTexture, pair.read.attach(0));
        blit(next);
        destroyTarget(pair.read);
        pair.read = next;
      } else {
        reshape(pair.read, width, height, format);
      }
      reshape(pair.write, width, height, format);
      pair.width = width;
      pair.height = height;
      pair.texelX = 1 / width;
      pair.texelY = 1 / height;
    };

    const resolution = base => {
      const width = gl.drawingBufferWidth;
      const height = gl.drawingBufferHeight;
      const aspect = Math.max(width, height) / Math.max(1, Math.min(width, height));
      const short = Math.round(base);
      const long = Math.round(base * aspect);
      return width > height ? { width: long, height: short } : { width: short, height: long };
    };

    const fit = () => {
      const ratio = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2);
      const width = Math.max(1, Math.floor(canvas.clientWidth * ratio));
      const height = Math.max(1, Math.floor(canvas.clientHeight * ratio));
      if (canvas.width === width && canvas.height === height) return;
      canvas.width = width;
      canvas.height = height;
    };

    const sizes = () => {
      const q = QUALITY[settingsRef.current.quality];
      const shortSide = Math.min(gl.drawingBufferWidth, gl.drawingBufferHeight);
      return { sim: resolution(q.sim), ink: resolution(Math.max(q.sim, Math.min(q.dye, shortSide))) };
    };

    fit();
    const initial = sizes();
    const dye = createDouble(initial.ink.width, initial.ink.height, rgba, gl.LINEAR);
    const velocity = createDouble(initial.sim.width, initial.sim.height, rg, gl.LINEAR);
    const pressure = createDouble(initial.sim.width, initial.sim.height, r, gl.NEAREST);
    const divergence = createTarget(initial.sim.width, initial.sim.height, r, gl.NEAREST);
    const curl = createTarget(initial.sim.width, initial.sim.height, r, gl.NEAREST);
    let bloom = null;
    let bloomChain = [];

    const dropBloom = () => {
      destroyTarget(bloom);
      bloomChain.forEach(destroyTarget);
      bloom = null;
      bloomChain = [];
    };

    const ensureBloom = () => {
      if (bloom) return bloom;
      const size = resolution(BLOOM_RESOLUTION);
      const created = createTarget(size.width, size.height, rgba, gl.LINEAR);
      for (let i = 0; i < BLOOM_ITERATIONS; i++) {
        const width = size.width >> (i + 1);
        const height = size.height >> (i + 1);
        if (width < 2 || height < 2) break;
        bloomChain.push(createTarget(width, height, rgba, gl.LINEAR));
      }
      bloom = created;
      return created;
    };

    const resize = () => {
      fit();
      const next = sizes();
      if (dye.width !== next.ink.width || dye.height !== next.ink.height) {
        resizeDouble(dye, next.ink.width, next.ink.height, rgba, gl.LINEAR, true);
      }
      if (velocity.width !== next.sim.width || velocity.height !== next.sim.height) {
        resizeDouble(velocity, next.sim.width, next.sim.height, rg, gl.LINEAR, true);
        resizeDouble(pressure, next.sim.width, next.sim.height, r, gl.NEAREST, false);
        reshape(divergence, next.sim.width, next.sim.height, r);
        reshape(curl, next.sim.width, next.sim.height, r);
      }
      const glowSize = resolution(BLOOM_RESOLUTION);
      if (bloom && (bloom.width !== glowSize.width || bloom.height !== glowSize.height)) dropBloom();
    };

    const radiusFor = scale => {
      const aspect = canvas.width / canvas.height;
      return ((0.2 * settingsRef.current.size * scale) / 100) * (aspect > 1 ? aspect : 1);
    };

    const splat = (ax, ay, bx, by, vx, vy, color, radius) => {
      const u = use('splat');
      gl.uniform1i(u.uTarget, velocity.read.attach(0));
      gl.uniform1f(u.aspectRatio, canvas.width / canvas.height);
      gl.uniform2f(u.pointA, ax, ay);
      gl.uniform2f(u.pointB, bx, by);
      gl.uniform3f(u.color, vx, vy, 0);
      gl.uniform1f(u.radius, radius);
      blit(velocity.write);
      velocity.swap();
      gl.uniform1i(u.uTarget, dye.read.attach(0));
      gl.uniform3f(u.color, color[0], color[1], color[2]);
      blit(dye.write);
      dye.swap();
    };

    const colorAt = (offset, now) => {
      const s = settingsRef.current;
      const phase = (((offset + (now / 1000) * s.colorSpeed * 0.6) % 1) + 1) % 1;
      if (s.rainbow) return hsv(phase, 1, 1);
      const palette = s.palette;
      if (palette.length === 1) return palette[0];
      const position = phase * palette.length;
      const from = palette[Math.floor(position) % palette.length];
      const to = palette[(Math.floor(position) + 1) % palette.length];
      const t = position - Math.floor(position);
      const blend = t * t * (3 - 2 * t);
      return [
        from[0] + (to[0] - from[0]) * blend,
        from[1] + (to[1] - from[1]) * blend,
        from[2] + (to[2] - from[2]) * blend
      ];
    };

    const inkFor = (color, amount) =>
      color.map(channel => (settingsRef.current.light ? 1 - channel : channel) * amount);

    const flush = (pointer, dt, now) => {
      const s = settingsRef.current;
      const aspect = canvas.width / canvas.height;
      const length = Math.hypot((pointer.x - pointer.px) * aspect, pointer.y - pointer.py);
      if (length > 0.35) {
        pointer.px = pointer.x;
        pointer.py = pointer.y;
        return;
      }
      let dx = pointer.x - pointer.px;
      let dy = pointer.y - pointer.py;
      if (aspect < 1) dx *= aspect;
      else dy /= aspect;
      const radius = radiusFor(1);
      const spread = 1 + length / (1.77 * Math.sqrt(radius));
      const push = (6000 * s.force) / Math.sqrt(spread);
      const amount = (0.15 * s.intensity * Math.min(dt * 60, 1)) / Math.sqrt(spread);
      const color = inkFor(colorAt(pointer.offset, now), amount);
      splat(pointer.px, pointer.py, pointer.x, pointer.y, dx * push, dy * push, color, radius);
      pointer.px = pointer.x;
      pointer.py = pointer.y;
    };

    const burst = (x, y, now) => {
      const s = settingsRef.current;
      const seed = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
      const aspect = canvas.width / canvas.height;
      let u = use('burst');
      gl.uniform1i(u.uTarget, velocity.read.attach(0));
      gl.uniform1f(u.aspectRatio, aspect);
      gl.uniform2f(u.point, x, y);
      gl.uniform1f(u.radius, radiusFor(3));
      gl.uniform1f(u.strength, 1100 * s.force);
      gl.uniform1f(u.seed, seed);
      blit(velocity.write);
      velocity.swap();
      const offset = Math.random();
      const amount = 1.6 * s.intensity;
      const first = inkFor(colorAt(offset, now), amount);
      const second = inkFor(colorAt(offset + 0.3, now), amount);
      u = use('crown');
      gl.uniform1i(u.uTarget, dye.read.attach(0));
      gl.uniform1f(u.aspectRatio, aspect);
      gl.uniform2f(u.point, x, y);
      gl.uniform1f(u.radius, radiusFor(1.6));
      gl.uniform3f(u.colorA, first[0], first[1], first[2]);
      gl.uniform3f(u.colorB, second[0], second[1], second[2]);
      gl.uniform1f(u.seed, seed);
      blit(dye.write);
      dye.swap();
    };

    const step = dt => {
      const s = settingsRef.current;
      const iterations = QUALITY[s.quality].iterations;
      gl.disable(gl.BLEND);

      let u = use('curl');
      gl.uniform2f(u.texelSize, velocity.texelX, velocity.texelY);
      gl.uniform1i(u.uVelocity, velocity.read.attach(0));
      blit(curl);

      u = use('vorticity');
      gl.uniform2f(u.texelSize, velocity.texelX, velocity.texelY);
      gl.uniform1i(u.uVelocity, velocity.read.attach(0));
      gl.uniform1i(u.uCurl, curl.attach(1));
      gl.uniform1f(u.curl, s.swirl);
      gl.uniform1f(u.dt, dt);
      blit(velocity.write);
      velocity.swap();

      u = use('divergence');
      gl.uniform2f(u.texelSize, velocity.texelX, velocity.texelY);
      gl.uniform1i(u.uVelocity, velocity.read.attach(0));
      blit(divergence);

      u = use('clear');
      gl.uniform1i(u.uTexture, pressure.read.attach(0));
      gl.uniform1f(u.value, 0.1);
      blit(pressure.write);
      pressure.swap();

      u = use('pressure');
      gl.uniform2f(u.texelSize, velocity.texelX, velocity.texelY);
      gl.uniform1i(u.uDivergence, divergence.attach(0));
      for (let i = 0; i < iterations; i++) {
        gl.uniform1i(u.uPressure, pressure.read.attach(1));
        blit(pressure.write);
        pressure.swap();
      }

      u = use('gradient');
      gl.uniform2f(u.texelSize, velocity.texelX, velocity.texelY);
      gl.uniform1i(u.uPressure, pressure.read.attach(0));
      gl.uniform1i(u.uVelocity, velocity.read.attach(1));
      blit(velocity.write);
      velocity.swap();

      u = use('advection');
      gl.uniform2f(u.texelSize, velocity.texelX, velocity.texelY);
      gl.uniform1i(u.uVelocity, velocity.read.attach(0));
      gl.uniform1i(u.uSource, velocity.read.attach(0));
      gl.uniform1f(u.dt, dt);
      gl.uniform1f(u.dissipation, 2 / s.momentum);
      blit(velocity.write);
      velocity.swap();

      gl.uniform1i(u.uVelocity, velocity.read.attach(0));
      gl.uniform1i(u.uSource, dye.read.attach(1));
      gl.uniform1f(u.dissipation, 3.5 / s.trail);
      blit(dye.write);
      dye.swap();
    };

    const applyBloom = amount => {
      const base = ensureBloom();
      if (!bloomChain.length) return;
      gl.disable(gl.BLEND);
      let u = use('prefilter');
      const knee = BLOOM_THRESHOLD * BLOOM_KNEE + 0.0001;
      gl.uniform3f(u.curve, BLOOM_THRESHOLD - knee, knee * 2, 0.25 / knee);
      gl.uniform1f(u.threshold, BLOOM_THRESHOLD);
      gl.uniform1i(u.uTexture, dye.read.attach(0));
      blit(base);
      u = use('blur');
      gl.uniform1f(u.intensity, 1);
      let last = base;
      bloomChain.forEach(target => {
        gl.uniform2f(u.texelSize, last.texelX, last.texelY);
        gl.uniform1i(u.uTexture, last.attach(0));
        blit(target);
        last = target;
      });
      gl.blendFunc(gl.ONE, gl.ONE);
      gl.enable(gl.BLEND);
      for (let i = bloomChain.length - 2; i >= 0; i--) {
        const target = bloomChain[i];
        gl.uniform2f(u.texelSize, last.texelX, last.texelY);
        gl.uniform1i(u.uTexture, last.attach(0));
        blit(target);
        last = target;
      }
      gl.disable(gl.BLEND);
      gl.uniform1f(u.intensity, amount);
      gl.uniform2f(u.texelSize, last.texelX, last.texelY);
      gl.uniform1i(u.uTexture, last.attach(0));
      blit(base);
    };

    const render = () => {
      const s = settingsRef.current;
      if (s.glow > 0) applyBloom(2 * s.glow);
      gl.disable(gl.BLEND);
      const u = use('display');
      gl.uniform2f(u.texelSize, 1 / gl.drawingBufferWidth, 1 / gl.drawingBufferHeight);
      gl.uniform1i(u.uTexture, dye.read.attach(0));
      gl.uniform1i(u.uBloom, (bloom ?? dye.read).attach(1));
      gl.uniform1f(u.shading, s.shading);
      gl.uniform1f(u.bloom, s.glow > 0 ? 1 : 0);
      gl.uniform1f(u.light, s.light ? 1 : 0);
      blit(null);
    };

    const clearScreen = () => {
      gl.bindFramebuffer(gl.FRAMEBUFFER, null);
      gl.viewport(0, 0, gl.drawingBufferWidth, gl.drawingBufferHeight);
      gl.clearColor(0, 0, 0, 0);
      gl.clear(gl.COLOR_BUFFER_BIT);
    };

    const pointers = new Map();
    const bursts = [];
    const reduced = window.matchMedia?.('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches ?? false;
    const introPointer = { x: 0, y: 0, px: 0, py: 0, ready: false, moved: false, offset: Math.random() };
    let introStart = introRef.current && !reduced ? performance.now() + 150 : -1;
    let raf = 0;
    let last = 0;
    let lastActivity = performance.now();

    const advanceIntro = now => {
      if (introStart < 0) return;
      const progress = (now - introStart) / 1100;
      if (progress < 0) return;
      const t = Math.min(progress, 1);
      const eased = t * t * (3 - 2 * t);
      const x = 0.2 + 0.6 * eased;
      const y = 0.5 + 0.12 * Math.sin(eased * Math.PI * 2);
      if (!introPointer.ready) {
        introPointer.x = introPointer.px = x;
        introPointer.y = introPointer.py = y;
        introPointer.ready = true;
      } else {
        introPointer.x = x;
        introPointer.y = y;
        introPointer.moved = true;
      }
      if (t >= 1) introStart = -1;
      lastActivity = now;
    };

    const hold = () => {
      const now = performance.now();
      if (introStart >= 0) introStart = now + 150;
      last = now;
      lastActivity = now;
      pointers.forEach(pointer => {
        pointer.px = pointer.x;
        pointer.py = pointer.y;
        pointer.moved = false;
      });
    };

    const frame = now => {
      raf = 0;
      if (disposed || lost) return;
      if (!ready) {
        if (gl.isContextLost()) return;
        if (!finish()) {
          hold();
          raf = requestAnimationFrame(frame);
          return;
        }
        if (broken) {
          dispose();
          return;
        }
      }
      const s = settingsRef.current;
      const dt = clamp((now - last) / 1000, 0, 1 / 60);
      last = now;
      resize();
      advanceIntro(now);
      if (introPointer.moved) {
        introPointer.moved = false;
        flush(introPointer, dt, now);
      }
      pointers.forEach(pointer => {
        if (!pointer.moved) return;
        pointer.moved = false;
        flush(pointer, dt, now);
      });
      bursts.splice(0).forEach(point => burst(point.x, point.y, now));
      step(dt);
      render();
      const quiet = (2.2 * s.trail + 0.4) * 1000;
      if (introStart >= 0 || now - lastActivity < quiet) raf = requestAnimationFrame(frame);
      else clearScreen();
    };

    const wake = () => {
      lastActivity = performance.now();
      if (raf || disposed || lost) return;
      last = performance.now();
      raf = requestAnimationFrame(frame);
    };

    const locate = (clientX, clientY) => {
      const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
      if (!rect.width || !rect.height) return null;
      const x = (clientX - rect.left) / rect.width;
      const y = 1 - (clientY - rect.top) / rect.height;
      if (settingsRef.current.contained && (x < 0 || x > 1 || y < 0 || y > 1)) return null;
      return { x, y };
    };

    const track = (id, clientX, clientY) => {
      const point = locate(clientX, clientY);
      let pointer = pointers.get(id);
      if (!point) {
        if (pointer) pointer.ready = false;
        return;
      }
      if (!pointer) {
        pointer = { x: 0, y: 0, px: 0, py: 0, ready: false, moved: false, offset: Math.random() };
        pointers.set(id, pointer);
      }
      if (!pointer.ready) {
        pointer.x = pointer.px = point.x;
        pointer.y = pointer.py = point.y;
        pointer.ready = true;
        return;
      }
      pointer.x = point.x;
      pointer.y = point.y;
      pointer.moved = true;
      wake();
    };

    const press = (clientX, clientY) => {
      if (!settingsRef.current.clickSplash) return;
      const point = locate(clientX, clientY);
      if (!point) return;
      bursts.push(point);
      wake();
    };

    const onPointerMove = event => {
      if (event.pointerType !== 'touch') track(`p${event.pointerId}`, event.clientX, event.clientY);
    };
    const onPointerDown = event => {
      if (event.pointerType !== 'touch') press(event.clientX, event.clientY);
    };
    const onTouchStart = event => {
      Array.from(event.changedTouches).forEach(touch => {
        pointers.delete(`t${touch.identifier}`);
        track(`t${touch.identifier}`, touch.clientX, touch.clientY);
        press(touch.clientX, touch.clientY);
      });
    };
    const onTouchMove = event => {
      Array.from(event.changedTouches).forEach(touch => track(`t${touch.identifier}`, touch.clientX, touch.clientY));
    };
    const onTouchEnd = event => {
      Array.from(event.changedTouches).forEach(touch => pointers.delete(`t${touch.identifier}`));
    };
    const onLeave = () => {
      pointers.forEach(pointer => {
        pointer.ready = false;
      });
    };
    const onLost = event => {
      event.preventDefault();
      lost = true;
      cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
      raf = 0;
    };
    const onRestored = () => setGeneration(value => value + 1);

    window.addEventListener('pointermove', onPointerMove, { passive: true });
    window.addEventListener('pointerdown', onPointerDown, { passive: true });
    window.addEventListener('touchstart', onTouchStart, { passive: true });
    window.addEventListener('touchmove', onTouchMove, { passive: true });
    window.addEventListener('touchend', onTouchEnd, { passive: true });
    window.addEventListener('touchcancel', onTouchEnd, { passive: true });
    document.documentElement.addEventListener('pointerleave', onLeave);
    canvas.addEventListener('webglcontextlost', onLost);
    canvas.addEventListener('webglcontextrestored', onRestored);

    const dispose = () => {
      if (disposed) return;
      disposed = true;
      cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
      window.removeEventListener('pointermove', onPointerMove);
      window.removeEventListener('pointerdown', onPointerDown);
      window.removeEventListener('touchstart', onTouchStart);
      window.removeEventListener('touchmove', onTouchMove);
      window.removeEventListener('touchend', onTouchEnd);
      window.removeEventListener('touchcancel', onTouchEnd);
      document.documentElement.removeEventListener('pointerleave', onLeave);
      canvas.removeEventListener('webglcontextlost', onLost);
      canvas.removeEventListener('webglcontextrestored', onRestored);
      release();
    };

    if (ready) resize();
    if (introStart >= 0) wake();

    return () => {
      dispose();
      releaseRef.current = window.setTimeout(() => {
        if (!canvas.isConnected && !gl.isContextLost()) gl.getExtension('WEBGL_lose_context')?.loseContext();
      }, 0);
    };
  }, [generation]);

  return (
    <div
      ref={rootRef}
      className={[ROOT, className].filter(Boolean).join(' ')}
      style={{ zIndex, ...style }}
      data-contained={contained ? '' : undefined}
      aria-hidden="true"
    >
      <canvas ref={canvasRef} className={CANVAS} />
    </div>
  );
}

export default SplashCursor;
```

```css
  inset: 0;
  pointer-events: none;

  position: absolute;
```

```jsx
import AcidSquares from './AcidSquares';

<div style={{ width: '100%', height: '600px', position: 'relative' }}>
  <AcidSquares
    color="#120f17"
    accentColor="#ff4fb8"
    secondaryColor="#3b6bff"
    tileSize={1}
    gap={0.12}
    roundness={0.25}
    rotation={35}
```

### Full Component Source
```jsx
'use client';

import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';

import './AcidSquares.css';

const MAX_RIPPLES = 8;
const MESH = { low: 150, medium: 210, high: 280 };
const AREA = [-42, -76, 84, 94];
const MAX_RENDER_DIM = 2400;
const INTRO_SECONDS = 3;
const REVEAL_SECONDS = 2.6;
const REVEAL_REACH = 19;
const ORIGIN = [0, -3];
const HOVER_EFFECTS = { press: 1, lift: -1, none: 0 };
const LIGHTING = {
  split: [
    [7, 4.2, -4, 4, 4],
    [-8, 3.6, 3, 4, 4]
  ],
  backlit: [
    [6, 1.6, -15, 5, 3],
    [-7, 1.4, -13, 5, 3]
  ],
  overhead: [
    [3, 7.5, -4, 3, 3],
    [-3, 7, -1, 3, 3]
  ]
};
const DEG = Math.PI / 180;

const FIELD = `
uniform vec4 uRipples[${MAX_RIPPLES}];
uniform vec4 uWave;
uniform float uSquare;
uniform float uClock;
uniform vec4 uSwell;
uniform vec4 uHover;
uniform vec4 uIntro;

float reachOf(vec2 delta, out vec2 grad) {
  vec2 a = abs(delta);
  vec2 dir = vec2(delta.x < 0.0 ? -1.0 : 1.0, delta.y < 0.0 ? -1.0 : 1.0);
  float round_ = length(delta);
  vec2 roundGrad = delta / max(round_, 1e-4);
  float m = max(max(a.x, a.y), 1e-4);
  vec2 k = a / m;
  vec2 k3 = k * k * k;
  float sum = dot(k3, k);
  float root = sqrt(sqrt(sum));
  float square = m * root;
  vec2 squareGrad = dir * k3 / (root * root * root);
  float d = mix(round_, square, uSquare);
  vec2 g = mix(roundGrad, squareGrad, uSquare);
  float soft = sqrt(d * d + 0.36);
  grad = g * d / soft;
  return soft - 0.6;
}

vec3 field(vec2 p) {
  float t = uSwell.w;
  float f = uSwell.y;
  vec2 k1 = vec2(0.19, 0.11) * f;
  vec2 k2 = vec2(-0.09, 0.23) * f;
  vec2 k3 = vec2(0.29, -0.17) * f;
  float a1 = dot(p, k1) + t * 0.55;
  float a2 = dot(p, k2) - t * 0.42 + 1.3;
  float a3 = dot(p, k3) + t * 0.74 + 2.1;
  float h = 0.55 * sin(a1) + 0.4 * sin(a2) + 0.22 * sin(a3);
  vec2 g = 0.55 * cos(a1) * k1 + 0.4 * cos(a2) * k2 + 0.22 * cos(a3) * k3;
  h *= uSwell.x;
  g *= uSwell.x;
  vec2 dg;
  for (int i = 0; i < ${MAX_RIPPLES}; i++) {
    vec4 r = uRipples[i];
    if (r.w <= 0.0) continue;
    float age = uClock - r.z;
    if (age <= 0.0) continue;
    float d = reachOf(p - r.xy, dg);
    float radius = age * uWave.y;
    float x = (d - radius) / uWave.z;
    float life = clamp(1.0 - radius / uWave.w, 0.0, 1.0);
    float strength = r.w * uWave.x * life * life * smoothstep(0.0, 1.5, radius);
    float bump = exp(-x * x);
    h += bump * strength;
    g += -2.0 * x * bump * strength / uWave.z * dg;
  }
  if (uIntro.w > 0.0) {
    float d = reachOf(p - uIntro.xy, dg);
    float x = (d - uIntro.z) / 2.2;
    float bump = exp(-x * x) * uIntro.w;
    h += bump;
    g += -2.0 * x * bump / 2.2 * dg;
  }
  vec2 hd = p - uHover.xy;
  float hr = max(uHover.z, 1e-3);
  float hover = exp(-dot(hd, hd) / (hr * hr)) * uHover.w;
  h -= hover;
  g += 2.0 * hd / (hr * hr) * hover;
  return vec3(h, g);
}
`;

const VERTEX = `#version 300 es
precision highp float;
${FIELD}
layout(location = 0) in vec2 aGrid;
uniform mat4 uViewProj;
uniform vec4 uArea;
out vec2 vGround;
out vec3 vWorld;
void main() {
  vec2 ground = uArea.xy + aGrid * uArea.zw;
  vec3 f = field(ground);
  vWorld = vec3(ground.x, f.x, ground.y);
  vGround = ground;
  gl_Position = uViewProj * vec4(vWorld, 1.0);
}`;

const FRAGMENT = `#version 300 es
precision highp float;
${FIELD}
uniform vec3 uEye;
uniform vec3 uColor;
uniform vec3 uAccent;
uniform vec3 uSecond;
uniform vec3 uLightA;
uniform vec3 uLightB;
uniform float uTile;
uniform float uAngle;
uniform float uGap;
uniform float uRound;
uniform float uSoftness;
uniform float uBulge;
uniform float uGloss;
uniform vec2 uRange;
uniform float uBrightness;
uniform float uGrain;
uniform float uGrainScale;
uniform float uLightMode;
uniform float uOpacity;
uniform vec4 uReveal;
uniform vec2 uView;
uniform float uFade;
uniform float uFrame;
in vec2 vGround;
in vec3 vWorld;
out vec4 outColor;

float smoother(float x) {
  x = clamp(x, 0.0, 1.0);
  return x * x * x * (x * (x * 6.0 - 15.0) + 10.0);
}

float hash(vec3 p) {
  p = fract(p * 0.1031);
  p += dot(p, p.zyx + 31.32);
  return fract((p.x + p.y) * p.z);
}

void main() {
  vec3 f = field(vGround);
  vec3 n0 = normalize(vec3(-f.y, 1.0, -f.z));
  vec3 tx = normalize(vec3(1.0, f.y, 0.0));
  vec3 tz = normalize(cross(tx, n0));
  tx = normalize(cross(n0, tz));

  float c = cos(uAngle);
  float s = sin(uAngle);
  mat2 toTile = mat2(c, -s, s, c);
  vec2 q = toTile * vGround / uTile;
  vec2 cell = floor(q);
  vec2 local = q - cell - 0.5;
  float footprint = max(length(fwidth(q)), 1e-5);

  float on = 1.0;
  if (uReveal.w < 1.0) {
    vec2 centre = transpose(toTile) * (cell + 0.5) * uTile;
    vec2 dg;
    float d = reachOf(centre - uReveal.xy, dg);
    float delay = hash(vec3(cell, 7.0)) * 1.1;
    on = max(smoother((uReveal.z - d - delay) / 1.6), uReveal.w);
  }

  float half_ = (0.5 - uGap * 0.5) * mix(0.62, 1.0, on);
  float radius = min(uRound * half_, half_);
  vec2 dd = abs(local) - (half_ - radius);
  float d = length(max(dd, 0.0)) + min(max(dd.x, dd.y), 0.0) - radius;
  vec2 outward = dd.x > 0.0 || dd.y > 0.0 ? normalize(max(dd, 0.0) * sign(local)) : (dd.x > dd.y ? vec2(sign(local.x), 0.0) : vec2(0.0, sign(local.y)));

  float bevelWidth = max(mix(0.04, 0.42, uSoftness) * half_, footprint * 1.5);
  float edge = clamp((d + bevelWidth) / bevelWidth, 0.0, 1.0);
  float dome = uBulge * 0.5 * length(local) / max(half_, 1e-3);
  float theta = edge * edge * mix(0.7, 1.35, uSoftness) + dome;
  vec2 outwardGround = transpose(toTile) * outward;
  vec2 domeGround = transpose(toTile) * (local / max(length(local), 1e-4));
  vec3 bend = normalize(tx * mix(domeGround.x, outwardGround.x, edge) + tz * mix(domeGround.y, outwardGround.y, edge));
  float crowd = smoothstep(0.08, 0.3, footprint);
  theta *= 1.0 - crowd;
  vec3 n = normalize(n0 * cos(theta) + bend * sin(theta));

  float inside = 1.0 - smoothstep(-footprint * 0.75, footprint * 0.75, d);
  float area = (2.0 * half_) * (2.0 * half_) - (4.0 - 3.14159) * radius * radius;
  inside = mix(inside, area, crowd) * on;

  vec3 toEye = uEye - vWorld;
  float dist = length(toEye);
  vec3 v = toEye / dist;
  vec3 la = uLightA - vWorld;
  vec3 lb = uLightB - vWorld;
  float da = length(la);
  float db = length(lb);
  la /= da;
  lb /= db;
  float fa = 1.0 / (1.0 + da * da * 0.02);
  float fb = 1.0 / (1.0 + db * db * 0.02);

  float ndv = clamp(dot(n, v), 0.0, 1.0);
  float fresnel = 0.04 + 0.96 * pow(1.0 - ndv, 5.0);
  vec3 ha = normalize(la + v);
  vec3 hb = normalize(lb + v);
  float shine = mix(10.0, 90.0, uGloss);
  float norm = (shine + 8.0) / 60.0;
  float specA = pow(max(dot(n, ha), 0.0), shine) * norm;
  float specB = pow(max(dot(n, hb), 0.0), shine) * norm;
  float sheenA = pow(max(dot(n, ha), 0.0), 8.0);
  float sheenB = pow(max(dot(n, hb), 0.0), 8.0);
  float diffA = max(dot(n, la), 0.0);
  float diffB = max(dot(n, lb), 0.0);
  vec3 r = reflect(-v, n);
  float rim = exp((dot(r, normalize(vec3(0.15, 0.55, -1.0))) - 1.0) * 6.0);
  float occlusion = mix(1.0, 0.35, edge * edge);
  float edgeLight = edge * edge;

  vec3 color;
  float alpha;
  if (uLightMode > 0.5) {
    float luma = dot(uColor, vec3(0.299, 0.587, 0.114));
    vec3 body = mix(vec3(luma), uColor, 0.5) * 0.96;
    vec3 keyDir = normalize(vec3(-0.35, 0.85, 0.45));
    float shade = clamp(dot(n, keyDir) * 0.6 + 0.5, 0.0, 1.0);
    float wa = clamp(diffA * fa * 9.0, 0.0, 1.0);
    float wb = clamp(diffB * fb * 9.0, 0.0, 1.0);
    vec3 hue = (uAccent * wa + uSecond * wb) / max(wa + wb, 1e-3);
    vec3 lit = body * mix(vec3(1.0), hue, clamp(wa + wb, 0.0, 1.0) * 0.42) * mix(0.7, 1.05, shade);
    lit += vec3(1.0) * (specA * fa + specB * fb) * 2.4 * uGloss;
    lit *= mix(1.0, 0.8, edgeLight * (1.0 - crowd));
    color = lit;
    alpha = inside;
  } else {
    float luma = dot(uColor, vec3(0.299, 0.587, 0.114));
    vec3 body = mix(vec3(luma), uColor, 0.35) + vec3(0.02);
    vec3 lit = body * 0.25;
    lit += body * (uAccent * diffA * fa + uSecond * diffB * fb) * 14.0;
    lit += uAccent * (specA * (0.55 + 1.6 * edgeLight) + sheenA * 0.3) * fa * 9.0 * uGloss;
    lit += uSecond * (specB * (0.55 + 1.6 * edgeLight) + sheenB * 0.3) * fb * 9.0 * uGloss;
    lit += mix(uSecond, vec3(1.0), 0.4) * rim * fresnel * 0.35 * uGloss;
    lit *= occlusion;
    color = lit * inside;
    alpha = inside;
  }

  float fade = 1.0 - smoother((dist - uRange.x) / max(uRange.y - uRange.x, 1e-3));
  alpha *= fade * uOpacity;
  color *= uBrightness;
  if (uLightMode < 0.5) color = 1.0 - exp(-color * 1.1);

  vec2 grainCell = floor(gl_FragCoord.xy / uGrainScale);
  float grain = hash(vec3(grainCell, uFrame)) + hash(vec3(grainCell + 17.0, uFrame + 3.0)) - 1.0;
  float lum = dot(color, vec3(0.299, 0.587, 0.114));
  color += grain * uGrain * (uLightMode > 0.5 ? 0.6 : 0.25 + 0.75 * sqrt(clamp(lum, 0.0, 1.0)));
  color = clamp(color, 0.0, 1.0);

  vec2 uv = gl_FragCoord.xy / uView;
  float edgeFade = max(uFade * 0.5, 1e-4);
  float mask = uFade > 0.0 ? smoother(uv.x / edgeFade) * smoother((1.0 - uv.x) / edgeFade) * smoother(uv.y / edgeFade) * smoother((1.0 - uv.y) / edgeFade) : 1.0;
  alpha *= mask;
  outColor = vec4(color * alpha, alpha);
}`;

const UNIFORMS = [
  'uRipples',
  'uWave',
  'uSquare',
  'uClock',
  'uSwell',
  'uHover',
  'uIntro',
  'uViewProj',
  'uArea',
  'uEye',
  'uColor',
  'uAccent',
  'uSecond',
  'uLightA',
  'uLightB',
  'uTile',
  'uAngle',
  'uGap',
  'uRound',
  'uSoftness',
  'uBulge',
  'uGloss',
  'uRange',
  'uBrightness',
  'uGrain',
  'uGrainScale',
  'uLightMode',
  'uOpacity',
  'uReveal',
  'uView',
  'uFade',
  'uFrame'
];

const clamp = (value, min, max) => Math.min(max, Math.max(min, value));

const ease = x => {
  const t = clamp(x, 0, 1);
  return 1 - (1 - t) * (1 - t) * (1 - t);
};

const glide = x => {
  const t = clamp(x, 0, 1);
  return t * t * t * (t * (t * 6 - 15) + 10);
};

const smoothstep = (a, b, x) => {
  const t = clamp((x - a) / (b - a), 0, 1);
  return t * t * (3 - 2 * t);
};

const compile = (gl, type, source) => {
  const shader = gl.createShader(type);
  if (!shader) return null;
  gl.shaderSource(shader, source);
  gl.compileShader(shader);
  if (gl.getShaderParameter(shader, gl.COMPILE_STATUS)) return shader;
  gl.deleteShader(shader);
  return null;
};

const link = (gl, vertexSource, fragmentSource) => {
  const vertex = compile(gl, gl.VERTEX_SHADER, vertexSource);
  const fragment = compile(gl, gl.FRAGMENT_SHADER, fragmentSource);
  const program = gl.createProgram();
  if (!vertex || !fragment || !program) return null;
  gl.attachShader(program, vertex);
  gl.attachShader(program, fragment);
  gl.linkProgram(program);
  gl.deleteShader(vertex);
  gl.deleteShader(fragment);
  if (!gl.getProgramParameter(program, gl.LINK_STATUS)) {
    gl.deleteProgram(program);
    return null;
  }
  const uniforms = {};
  for (const name of UNIFORMS) uniforms[name] = gl.getUniformLocation(program, name);
  return { program, uniforms };
};

const perspective = (fov, aspect, near, far) => {
  const f = 1 / Math.tan(fov / 2);
  const nf = 1 / (near - far);
  return [f / aspect, 0, 0, 0, 0, f, 0, 0, 0, 0, (far + near) * nf, -1, 0, 0, 2 * far * near * nf, 0];
};

const lookAt = (eye, target) => {
  let zx = eye[0] - target[0];
  let zy = eye[1] - target[1];
  let zz = eye[2] - target[2];
  let len = Math.hypot(zx, zy, zz) || 1;
  zx /= len;
  zy /= len;
  zz /= len;
  let xx = zz;
  let xz = -zx;
  len = Math.hypot(xx, xz) || 1;
  xx /= len;
  xz /= len;
  const yx = zy * xz;
  const yy = zz * xx - zx * xz;
  const yz = -zy * xx;
  return [
    xx,
    yx,
    zx,
    0,
    0,
    yy,
    zy,
    0,
    xz,
    yz,
    zz,
    0,
    -(xx * eye[0] + xz * eye[2]),
    -(yx * eye[0] + yy * eye[1] + yz * eye[2]),
    -(zx * eye[0] + zy * eye[1] + zz * eye[2]),
    1
  ];
};

const multiply = (a, b) => {
  const out = new Array(16);
  for (let col = 0; col < 4; col += 1) {
    for (let row = 0; row < 4; row += 1) {
      out[col * 4 + row] =
        a[row] * b[col * 4] + a[4 + row] * b[col * 4 + 1] + a[8 + row] * b[col * 4 + 2] + a[12 + row] * b[col * 4 + 3];
    }
  }
  return out;
};

const invert = a => {
  const b00 = a[0] * a[5] - a[1] * a[4];
  const b01 = a[0] * a[6] - a[2] * a[4];
  const b02 = a[0] * a[7] - a[3] * a[4];
  const b03 = a[1] * a[6] - a[2] * a[5];
  const b04 = a[1] * a[7] - a[3] * a[5];
  const b05 = a[2] * a[7] - a[3] * a[6];
  const b06 = a[8] * a[13] - a[9] * a[12];
  const b07 = a[8] * a[14] - a[10] * a[12];
  const b08 = a[8] * a[15] - a[11] * a[12];
  const b09 = a[9] * a[14] - a[10] * a[13];
  const b10 = a[9] * a[15] - a[11] * a[13];
  const b11 = a[10] * a[15] - a[11] * a[14];
  const det = b00 * b11 - b01 * b10 + b02 * b09 + b03 * b08 - b04 * b07 + b05 * b06;
  const k = det ? 1 / det : 0;
  return [
    (a[5] * b11 - a[6] * b10 + a[7] * b09) * k,
    (a[2] * b10 - a[1] * b11 - a[3] * b09) * k,
    (a[13] * b05 - a[14] * b04 + a[15] * b03) * k,
    (a[10] * b04 - a[9] * b05 - a[11] * b03) * k,
    (a[6] * b08 - a[4] * b11 - a[7] * b07) * k,
    (a[0] * b11 - a[2] * b08 + a[3] * b07) * k,
    (a[14] * b02 - a[12] * b05 - a[15] * b01) * k,
    (a[8] * b05 - a[10] * b02 + a[11] * b01) * k,
    (a[4] * b10 - a[5] * b08 + a[7] * b06) * k,
    (a[1] * b08 - a[0] * b10 - a[3] * b06) * k,
    (a[12] * b04 - a[13] * b02 + a[15] * b00) * k,
    (a[9] * b02 - a[8] * b04 - a[11] * b00) * k,
    (a[5] * b07 - a[4] * b09 - a[6] * b06) * k,
    (a[0] * b09 - a[1] * b07 + a[2] * b06) * k,
    (a[13] * b01 - a[12] * b03 - a[14] * b00) * k,
    (a[8] * b03 - a[9] * b01 + a[10] * b00) * k
  ];
};

const AcidSquares = ({
  color = '#120f17',
  accentColor = '#ff4fb8',
  secondaryColor = '#3b6bff',
  tileSize = 1,
  gap = 0.12,
  roundness = 0.25,
  softness = 0.5,
  bulge = 0.3,
  rotation = 35,
  gloss = 0.6,
  waves = 1,
  waveScale = 1,
  speed = 0.6,
  shape = 'square',
  pulse = 1,
  pulseSpeed = 0.45,
  pulseSpacing = 1,
  pulseWidth = 1,
  pulseOrigin = 'center',
  brightness = 1,
  lighting = 'split',
  tilt = 0.7,
  zoom = 1,
  depth = 10,
  grain = 0.12,
  fade = 0,
  opacity = 1,
  intro = true,
  mouseInteraction = true,
  hoverEffect = 'press',
  mouseStrength = 1,
  mouseRadius = 0.35,
  pulseOnClick = true,
  detail = 'medium',
  lightMode = false,
  paused = false,
  dpr,
  ...rest
}) => {
  const containerRef = useRef(null);
  const canvasRef = useRef(null);
  const wakeRef = useRef(null);
  const settings = {
    color: String(color),
    accentColor: String(accentColor),
    secondaryColor: String(secondaryColor),
    tileSize: clamp(tileSize, 0.2, 5),
    gap: clamp(gap, 0, 0.6),
    roundness: clamp(roundness, 0, 1),
    softness: clamp(softness, 0, 1),
    bulge: clamp(bulge, 0, 1.5),
    rotation: rotation * DEG,
    gloss: clamp(gloss, 0, 1),
    waves: Math.max(0, waves),
    waveScale: clamp(waveScale, 0.1, 5),
    speed: Math.max(0, speed),
    square: shape === 'round' ? 0 : 1,
    pulse: Math.max(0, pulse),
    pulseSpeed: Math.max(0, pulseSpeed),
    pulseSpacing: clamp(pulseSpacing, 0.2, 6),
    pulseWidth: clamp(pulseWidth, 0.1, 5),
    randomOrigin: pulseOrigin === 'random',
    brightness: Math.max(0, brightness),
    lights: LIGHTING[lighting] || LIGHTING.split,
    tilt: clamp(tilt, 0, 1),
    zoom: clamp(zoom, 0.3, 4),
    depth: clamp(depth, 2, 40),
    grain: Math.max(0, grain),
    fade: clamp(fade, 0, 1),
    opacity: clamp(opacity, 0, 1),
    intro,
    mouseInteraction,
    hover: HOVER_EFFECTS[hoverEffect] ?? 1,
    mouseStrength: Math.max(0, mouseStrength),
    mouseRadius: clamp(mouseRadius, 0.02, 2),
    pulseOnClick,
    mesh: MESH[detail] || MESH.medium,
    lightMode,
    paused,
    dpr
  };
  const settingsRef = useRef(settings);
  settingsRef.current = settings;

  useEffect(() => {
    const container = containerRef.current;
    const canvas = canvasRef.current;
    const gl = canvas?.getContext('webgl2', {
      alpha: true,
      premultipliedAlpha: true,
      antialias: true,
      depth: true,
      stencil: false
    });
    if (!container || !canvas || !gl) return undefined;

    const surface = link(gl, VERTEX, FRAGMENT);
    if (!surface) return undefined;

    const vao = gl.createVertexArray();
    const gridBuffer = gl.createBuffer();
    const indexBuffer = gl.createBuffer();
    let meshSize = 0;
    let indexCount = 0;
    const buildMesh = size => {
      if (size === meshSize) return;
      meshSize = size;
      const points = new Float32Array((size + 1) * (size + 1) * 2);
      let o = 0;
      for (let j = 0; j <= size; j += 1) {
        for (let i = 0; i <= size; i += 1) {
          points[o] = i / size;
          points[o + 1] = j / size;
          o += 2;
        }
      }
      const indices = new Uint32Array(size * size * 6);
      o = 0;
      for (let j = 0; j < size; j += 1) {
        for (let i = 0; i < size; i += 1) {
          const a = j * (size + 1) + i;
          const b = a + 1;
          const c = a + size + 1;
          const d = c + 1;
          indices[o] = a;
          indices[o + 1] = c;
          indices[o + 2] = b;
          indices[o + 3] = b;
          indices[o + 4] = c;
          indices[o + 5] = d;
          o += 6;
        }
      }
      indexCount = indices.length;
      gl.bindVertexArray(vao);
      gl.bindBuffer(gl.ARRAY_BUFFER, gridBuffer);
      gl.bufferData(gl.ARRAY_BUFFER, points, gl.STATIC_DRAW);
      gl.enableVertexAttribArray(0);
      gl.vertexAttribPointer(0, 2, gl.FLOAT, false, 0, 0);
      gl.bindBuffer(gl.ELEMENT_ARRAY_BUFFER, indexBuffer);
      gl.bufferData(gl.ELEMENT_ARRAY_BUFFER, indices, gl.STATIC_DRAW);
      gl.bindVertexArray(null);
    };

    const probe = document.createElement('canvas');
    probe.width = 1;
    probe.height = 1;
    const probeContext = probe.getContext('2d', { willReadFrequently: true });
    const reduce = window.matchMedia?.('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches ?? false;
    const state = {
      clock: 0,
      swell: 0,
      lights: 0,
      frame: 0,
      nextRipple: 1.2,
      ripples: [],
      intro: settingsRef.current.intro && !reduce ? 0 : INTRO_SECONDS,
      width: 1,
      height: 1,
      colorKey: '',
      color: [0.07, 0.06, 0.09],
      accent: [1, 0.31, 0.72],
      second: [0.23, 0.42, 1],
      pointer: { x: 0, y: 0, inside: false },
      hover: { x: 0, z: 0, vx: 0, vz: 0, presence: 0, placed: false },
      look: { x: 0, y: 0, vx: 0, vy: 0 },
      seed: 1,
      camera: null
    };
    let raf = 0;
    let last = 0;
    let visible = true;
    let alive = true;

    const toRgb = (value, fallback) => {
      if (!probeContext || !value) return fallback;
      const css = /^[0-9a-f]{3,8}$/i.test(value) ? `#${value}` : value;
      probeContext.clearRect(0, 0, 1, 1);
      probeContext.fillStyle = '#000000';
      probeContext.fillStyle = css;
      probeContext.fillRect(0, 0, 1, 1);
      const [r, g, b] = probeContext.getImageData(0, 0, 1, 1).data;
      return [r / 255, g / 255, b / 255];
    };

    const resolveColors = s => {
      const key = `${s.color}|${s.accentColor}|${s.secondaryColor}`;
      if (key === state.colorKey) return;
      state.colorKey = key;
      state.color = toRgb(s.color, [0.07, 0.06, 0.09]);
      state.accent = toRgb(s.accentColor, [1, 0.31, 0.72]);
      state.second = toRgb(s.secondaryColor, [0.23, 0.42, 1]);
    };

    const random = () => {
      state.seed = (state.seed * 16807) % 2147483647;
      return (state.seed - 1) / 2147483646;
    };

    const cameraFor = s => {
      const aspect = state.width / Math.max(state.height, 1);
      const distance = 8 / s.zoom;
      const yaw = state.look.x * 0.16;
      const pitch = 1.5 - s.tilt * 1.26 + state.look.y * 0.05;
      const target = [ORIGIN[0], 0, ORIGIN[1]];
      const eye = [
        target[0] + Math.sin(yaw) * Math.cos(pitch) * distance,
        target[1] + Math.sin(pitch) * distance,
        target[2] + Math.cos(yaw) * Math.cos(pitch) * distance
      ];
      const projection = perspective(0.8, aspect, 0.1, 400);
      const view = lookAt(eye, target);
      const viewProj = multiply(projection, view);
      return { eye, target, viewProj, inverse: invert(viewProj), distance };
    };

    const rippleData = new Float32Array(MAX_RIPPLES * 4);

    const render = () => {
      const s = settingsRef.current;
      buildMesh(s.mesh);
      resolveColors(s);
      const camera = cameraFor(s);
      state.camera = camera;
      gl.viewport(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
      gl.clearColor(0, 0, 0, 0);
      gl.clearDepth(1);
      gl.clear(gl.COLOR_BUFFER_BIT | gl.DEPTH_BUFFER_BIT);
      gl.enable(gl.DEPTH_TEST);
      gl.depthFunc(gl.LEQUAL);
      gl.useProgram(surface.program);
      const u = surface.uniforms;
      rippleData.fill(0);
      state.ripples.forEach((ripple, index) => {
        rippleData[index * 4] = ripple.x;
        rippleData[index * 4 + 1] = ripple.z;
        rippleData[index * 4 + 2] = ripple.start;
        rippleData[index * 4 + 3] = ripple.strength;
      });
      const introAge = state.intro;
      const revealing = introAge < INTRO_SECONDS;
      const revealRadius = glide(introAge / REVEAL_SECONDS) * REVEAL_REACH;
      const settle = ease(introAge / INTRO_SECONDS);
      gl.uniform4fv(u.uRipples, rippleData);
      gl.uniform4f(u.uWave, 0.55 * s.pulse, 3 * Math.max(s.pulseSpeed, 0.05) + 1.2, 1.6 * s.pulseWidth, 46);
      gl.uniform1f(u.uSquare, s.square);
      gl.uniform1f(u.uClock, state.clock);
      gl.uniform4f(u.uSwell, 1.3 * s.waves * (0.25 + 0.75 * settle), 1.9 / s.waveScale, 0, state.swell);
      gl.uniform4f(
        u.uHover,
        state.hover.x,
        state.hover.z,
        (3 * s.mouseRadius) / 0.35,
        0.7 * state.hover.presence * s.mouseStrength * s.hover
      );
      gl.uniform4f(
        u.uIntro,
        ORIGIN[0],
        ORIGIN[1],
        revealRadius,
        revealing ? 0.9 * (1 - smoothstep(0.55, 1, introAge / INTRO_SECONDS)) : 0
      );
      gl.uniformMatrix4fv(u.uViewProj, false, camera.viewProj);
      gl.uniform4f(u.uArea, AREA[0] + ORIGIN[0], AREA[1] + ORIGIN[1], AREA[2], AREA[3]);
      gl.uniform3f(u.uEye, camera.eye[0], camera.eye[1], camera.eye[2]);
      gl.uniform3f(u.uColor, state.color[0], state.color[1], state.color[2]);
      gl.uniform3f(u.uAccent, state.accent[0], state.accent[1], state.accent[2]);
      gl.uniform3f(u.uSecond, state.second[0], state.second[1], state.second[2]);
      const orbit = state.lights;
      const reach = camera.distance / 8;
      const lightLevel = 0.35 + 0.65 * settle;
      const [first, second] = s.lights;
      gl.uniform3f(
        u.uLightA,
        camera.target[0] + (first[0] + Math.sin(orbit * 0.7) * first[3]) * reach,
        first[1] * reach * lightLevel + 0.6,
        camera.target[2] + (first[2] + Math.cos(orbit * 0.5) * first[4]) * reach
      );
      gl.uniform3f(
        u.uLightB,
        camera.target[0] + (second[0] + Math.cos(orbit * 0.6) * second[3]) * reach,
        second[1] * reach * lightLevel + 0.6,
        camera.target[2] + (second[2] + Math.sin(orbit * 0.45) * second[4]) * reach
      );
      gl.uniform1f(u.uTile, 1.2 * s.tileSize);
      gl.uniform1f(u.uAngle, s.rotation);
      gl.uniform1f(u.uGap, s.gap);
      gl.uniform1f(u.uRound, s.roundness);
      gl.uniform1f(u.uSoftness, s.softness);
      gl.uniform1f(u.uBulge, s.bulge);
      gl.uniform1f(u.uGloss, s.gloss);
      const far = Math.min(s.depth * 3.2 * reach, AREA[3] * 0.62);
      gl.uniform2f(u.uRange, far * 0.5, far);
      gl.uniform1f(u.uBrightness, s.brightness);
      const pixelRatio = canvas.height / Math.max(state.height, 1);
      gl.uniform1f(u.uGrain, s.grain);
      gl.uniform1f(u.uGrainScale, Math.max(1, pixelRatio));
      gl.uniform1f(u.uLightMode, s.lightMode ? 1 : 0);
      gl.uniform1f(u.uOpacity, s.opacity);
      gl.uniform4f(u.uReveal, ORIGIN[0], ORIGIN[1], revealRadius, revealing ? 0 : 1);
      gl.uniform2f(u.uView, canvas.width, canvas.height);
      gl.uniform1f(u.uFade, s.fade);
      gl.uniform1f(u.uFrame, state.frame % 977);
      gl.bindVertexArray(vao);
      gl.drawElements(gl.TRIANGLES, indexCount, gl.UNSIGNED_INT, 0);
      gl.bindVertexArray(null);
      gl.disable(gl.DEPTH_TEST);
    };

    const resize = () => {
      const s = settingsRef.current;
      const width = Math.max(1, container.clientWidth);
      const height = Math.max(1, container.clientHeight);
      const baseDpr = Math.min(s.dpr || window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2);
      const longest = Math.max(width, height) * baseDpr;
      const ratio = longest > MAX_RENDER_DIM ? (baseDpr * MAX_RENDER_DIM) / longest : baseDpr;
      const pixelWidth = Math.max(1, Math.round(width * ratio));
      const pixelHeight = Math.max(1, Math.round(height * ratio));
      if (canvas.width !== pixelWidth || canvas.height !== pixelHeight) {
        canvas.width = pixelWidth;
        canvas.height = pixelHeight;
      }
      state.width = width;
      state.height = height;
      render();
    };

    const groundPoint = (px, py) => {
      const camera = state.camera;
      if (!camera) return null;
      const nx = (px / state.width) * 2 - 1;
      const ny = 1 - (py / state.height) * 2;
      const m = camera.inverse;
      const fx = m[0] * nx + m[4] * ny + m[8] + m[12];
      const fy = m[1] * nx + m[5] * ny + m[9] + m[13];
      const fz = m[2] * nx + m[6] * ny + m[10] + m[14];
      const fw = m[3] * nx + m[7] * ny + m[11] + m[15];
      const dx = fx / fw - camera.eye[0];
      const dy = fy / fw - camera.eye[1];
      const dz = fz / fw - camera.eye[2];
      if (dy >= -1e-4) return null;
      const t = -camera.eye[1] / dy;
      return { x: camera.eye[0] + dx * t, z: camera.eye[2] + dz * t };
    };

    const spawn = (x, z, strength) => {
      state.ripples.push({ x, z, start: state.clock, strength });
      while (state.ripples.length > MAX_RIPPLES) state.ripples.shift();
    };

    const frame = now => {
      raf = 0;
      if (!alive || !visible || document.hidden) return;
      const s = settingsRef.current;
      const dt = last ? Math.min(0.05, (now - last) / 1000) : 1 / 60;
      last = now;
      const moving = !s.paused && !reduce;
      if (moving) {
        state.frame += 1;
        state.clock += dt;
        state.intro += dt;
        state.swell += dt * s.speed;
        state.lights += dt * s.speed * 0.6;
        const travel = 3 * Math.max(s.pulseSpeed, 0.05) + 1.2;
        const interval = (10 * s.pulseSpacing) / travel;
        if (s.pulse > 0 && state.clock >= state.nextRipple) {
          if (s.randomOrigin) {
            const spot = groundPoint((0.15 + random() * 0.7) * state.width, (0.25 + random() * 0.6) * state.height);
            if (spot) spawn(spot.x, spot.z, 1);
          } else {
            spawn(ORIGIN[0], ORIGIN[1], 1);
          }
          state.nextRipple = state.clock + interval * (s.randomOrigin ? 0.6 : 1);
        }
        const lifetime = 46 / travel + 1;
        state.ripples = state.ripples.filter(ripple => state.clock - ripple.start < lifetime);
      } else if (state.intro < INTRO_SECONDS) {
        state.intro = INTRO_SECONDS;
      }

      const { pointer, hover, look } = state;
      const active = s.mouseInteraction && pointer.inside && !reduce;
      const spot = active && s.hover !== 0 ? groundPoint(pointer.x, pointer.y) : null;
      if (spot) {
        if (!hover.placed) {
          hover.x = spot.x;
          hover.z = spot.z;
          hover.vx = 0;
          hover.vz = 0;
          hover.placed = true;
        }
        hover.vx += (90 * (spot.x - hover.x) - 19 * hover.vx) * dt;
        hover.vz += (90 * (spot.z - hover.z) - 19 * hover.vz) * dt;
        hover.x += hover.vx * dt;
        hover.z += hover.vz * dt;
      }
      const goal = spot ? 1 : 0;
      hover.presence += (goal - hover.presence) * (1 - Math.exp(-dt / (goal > hover.presence ? 0.35 : 0.6)));
      if (!spot && hover.presence < 0.001) hover.placed = false;
      const lookX = active ? (pointer.x / state.width - 0.5) * 0.5 : 0;
      const lookY = active ? (0.5 - pointer.y / state.height) * 0.5 : 0;
      look.vx += (30 * (lookX - look.x) - 11 * look.vx) * dt;
      look.vy += (30 * (lookY - look.y) - 11 * look.vy) * dt;
      look.x += look.vx * dt;
      look.y += look.vy * dt;

      render();
      const settling =
        Math.abs(goal - hover.presence) > 0.002 ||
        Math.hypot(hover.vx, hover.vz) > 0.01 ||
        Math.abs(lookX - look.x) > 0.0002 ||
        Math.abs(lookY - look.y) > 0.0002;
      if (moving || settling) raf = requestAnimationFrame(frame);
      else last = 0;
    };

    const wake = () => {
      if (!raf && alive && visible && !document.hidden) raf = requestAnimationFrame(frame);
    };

    const onPointerMove = event => {
      const rect = container.getBoundingClientRect();
      const x = event.clientX - rect.left;
      const y = event.clientY - rect.top;
      state.pointer.x = x;
      state.pointer.y = y;
      state.pointer.inside = x >= 0 && y >= 0 && x <= rect.width && y <= rect.height;
      wake();
    };

    const onPointerDown = event => {
      const s = settingsRef.current;
      if (!s.mouseInteraction || !s.pulseOnClick || event.button !== 0 || reduce) return;
      if (
        event.target instanceof Element &&
        event.target.closest('a, button, input, textarea, select, label, [role="button"]')
      )
        return;
      const rect = container.getBoundingClientRect();
      const x = event.clientX - rect.left;
      const y = event.clientY - rect.top;
      if (x < 0 || y < 0 || x > rect.width || y > rect.height) return;
      const spot = groundPoint(x, y);
      if (spot) spawn(spot.x, spot.z, 1.15);
      wake();
    };

    const onPointerLeave = () => {
      state.pointer.inside = false;
      wake();
    };

    const onVisibility = () => {
      last = 0;
      wake();
    };

    const resizeObserver = new ResizeObserver(resize);
    resizeObserver.observe(container);
    const intersection = new IntersectionObserver(entries => {
      visible = entries.some(entry => entry.isIntersecting);
      if (visible) {
        last = 0;
        wake();
      }
    });
    intersection.observe(container);
    window.addEventListener('pointermove', onPointerMove, { passive: true });
    window.addEventListener('pointerdown', onPointerDown);
    document.documentElement.addEventListener('pointerleave', onPointerLeave);
    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', onVisibility);
    resize();
    wake();

    wakeRef.current = () => {
      if (!raf) render();
      wake();
    };

    return () => {
      alive = false;
      cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
      wakeRef.current = null;
      resizeObserver.disconnect();
      intersection.disconnect();
      window.removeEventListener('pointermove', onPointerMove);
      window.removeEventListener('pointerdown', onPointerDown);
      document.documentElement.removeEventListener('pointerleave', onPointerLeave);
      document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', onVisibility);
      gl.deleteBuffer(gridBuffer);
      gl.deleteBuffer(indexBuffer);
      gl.deleteVertexArray(vao);
      gl.deleteProgram(surface.program);
    };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    wakeRef.current?.();
  });

  const { className = '', ...attributes } = rest;

  return (
    <div ref={containerRef} className={`acid-squares-container${className ? ` ${className}` : ''}`} {...attributes}>
      <canvas ref={canvasRef} className="acid-squares-canvas" aria-hidden="true" />
    </div>
  );
};

export default AcidSquares;
```
