### Full Component Source
```jsx
'use client';

import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';

const FOV = 50;
const CAMERA_Z = 20;
const MAX_ASPECT = 1.5;
const MAX_SPEED = 16;
const STRIDE = 12;
const SLOP = 0.01;

const MATERIALS = {
  satin: { roughness: 0.42, metalness: 0, coat: 0.35, coatRoughness: 0.22, scatter: 1, frost: 0 },
  matte: { roughness: 0.92, metalness: 0, coat: 0, coatRoughness: 0.5, scatter: 0.5, frost: 0 },
  gloss: { roughness: 0.2, metalness: 0, coat: 1, coatRoughness: 0.08, scatter: 1, frost: 0 },
  frosted: { roughness: 0.55, metalness: 0, coat: 0.6, coatRoughness: 0.18, scatter: 1.5, frost: 1 },
  metal: { roughness: 0.3, metalness: 1, coat: 0, coatRoughness: 0.2, scatter: 0, frost: 0 }
};

const VERTEX = `#version 300 es
precision highp float;
layout(location = 0) in vec2 aCorner;
layout(location = 1) in vec4 aSphere;
layout(location = 2) in vec4 aColor;
layout(location = 3) in vec4 aShade;
uniform mat4 uProj;
uniform float uCameraZ;
uniform float uPixel;
out vec3 vPoint;
flat out vec3 vCenter;
flat out float vRadius;
flat out vec4 vColor;
flat out vec4 vShade;
void main() {
  vec3 c = vec3(aSphere.xy, aSphere.z - uCameraZ);
  float r = aSphere.w;
  float d = length(c);
  vec3 toCamera = -c / d;
  vec3 right = normalize(cross(vec3(0.0, 1.0, 0.0), toCamera));
  vec3 up = cross(toCamera, right);
  float reach = r * d / sqrt(max(d * d - r * r, 1e-4)) + uPixel * d * 2.0;
  vec3 p = c + (right * aCorner.x + up * aCorner.y) * reach;
  vPoint = p;
  vCenter = c;
  vRadius = r;
  vColor = aColor;
  vShade = aShade;
  gl_Position = uProj * vec4(p, 1.0);
}`;

const FRAGMENT = `#version 300 es
precision highp float;
in vec3 vPoint;
flat in vec3 vCenter;
flat in float vRadius;
flat in vec4 vColor;
flat in vec4 vShade;
uniform mat4 uProj;
uniform vec3 uLightPos;
uniform vec3 uLight;
uniform float uExposure;
uniform float uShadow;
uniform vec4 uSurface;
uniform vec2 uExtra;
out vec4 outColor;

const float PI = 3.14159265;

float lobe(vec3 d, vec3 axis, float sharp) {
  return pow(max(dot(d, axis), 0.0), sharp) * (sharp + 1.0) * 0.16;
}

vec3 studio(vec3 d, float rough) {
  float r = clamp(rough, 0.0, 1.0);
  float sharp = mix(60.0, 1.2, sqrt(r));
  vec3 col = mix(vec3(0.085, 0.085, 0.09), vec3(0.38, 0.38, 0.39), smoothstep(-0.7, 0.9, d.y));
  col += vec3(1.0, 0.99, 0.97) * 2.6 * lobe(d, normalize(vec3(-0.42, 0.78, 0.47)), sharp);
  col += vec3(0.97, 0.98, 1.0) * 1.1 * lobe(d, normalize(vec3(0.78, 0.22, 0.58)), sharp * 0.7);
  col += vec3(1.0) * 1.3 * lobe(d, normalize(vec3(0.15, 0.55, -0.82)), sharp * 0.8);
  return col;
}

vec2 envBRDF(float NoV, float rough) {
  vec4 c0 = vec4(-1.0, -0.0275, -0.572, 0.022);
  vec4 c1 = vec4(1.0, 0.0425, 1.04, -0.04);
  vec4 r = rough * c0 + c1;
  float a = min(r.x * r.x, exp2(-9.28 * NoV)) * r.x + r.y;
  return vec2(-1.04, 1.04) * a + r.zw;
}

float ggx(float NoH, float a) {
  float a2 = a * a;
  float d = NoH * NoH * (a2 - 1.0) + 1.0;
  return a2 / (PI * d * d);
}

float smith(float NoV, float NoL, float a) {
  float k = a * 0.5;
  return 0.25 / max((NoV * (1.0 - k) + k) * (NoL * (1.0 - k) + k), 1e-4);
}

vec3 aces(vec3 x) {
  return clamp((x * (2.51 * x + 0.03)) / (x * (2.43 * x + 0.59) + 0.14), 0.0, 1.0);
}

void main() {
  vec3 rd = normalize(vPoint);
  float b = dot(vCenter, rd);
  float h = length(vCenter - rd * b);
  float r = vRadius;
  float aa = max(fwidth(h), 1e-5);
  float cover = clamp((r - h) / aa + 0.5, 0.0, 1.0);
  if (cover <= 0.002) discard;
  float hc = min(h, r * 0.9995);
  float t = b - sqrt(r * r - hc * hc);
  vec3 pos = rd * t;
  vec3 n = normalize(pos - vCenter);
  vec4 clip = uProj * vec4(pos, 1.0);
  gl_FragDepth = clamp(clip.z / clip.w * 0.5 + 0.5, 0.0, 1.0);

  vec3 v = -rd;
  vec3 albedo = vColor.rgb;
  bool lamp = vColor.a > 0.5;
  vec3 dn = fwidth(n);
  float rough = sqrt(min(uSurface.x * uSurface.x + dot(dn, dn) * 0.6, 1.0));
  float metal = uSurface.y;
  float coat = uSurface.z;
  float coatRough = sqrt(min(uSurface.w * uSurface.w + dot(dn, dn) * 0.6, 1.0));
  float NoV = clamp(dot(n, v), 1e-4, 1.0);
  vec3 R = reflect(-v, n);

  float occlusion = vShade.w * smoothstep(-0.15, 0.9, dot(n, vShade.xyz));
  float ao = 1.0 - uShadow * occlusion;
  float specAo = 1.0 - uShadow * occlusion * 0.7;

  vec3 L = uLightPos - pos;
  float dist2 = dot(L, L);
  L *= inversesqrt(dist2);
  vec3 radiance = lamp ? vec3(0.0) : uLight / (dist2 + 1.0);
  float NoL = clamp(dot(n, L), 0.0, 1.0);
  vec3 H = normalize(L + v);
  float NoH = clamp(dot(n, H), 0.0, 1.0);
  float VoH = clamp(dot(v, H), 0.0, 1.0);

  vec3 F0 = mix(vec3(0.04), albedo, metal);
  vec2 ab = envBRDF(NoV, rough);
  vec3 Fr = max(vec3(1.0 - rough), F0) - F0;
  vec3 kS = F0 + Fr * pow(1.0 - NoV, 5.0);
  vec3 diffuseColor = albedo * (1.0 - metal);
  vec3 irradiance = studio(n, 1.0) * 1.15;
  vec3 envSpec = studio(R, rough) * (F0 * ab.x + ab.y);
  vec3 col = diffuseColor * irradiance * (1.0 - kS) * ao + envSpec * specAo;
  vec3 F = F0 + (1.0 - F0) * pow(1.0 - VoH, 5.0);
  float a = max(rough * rough, 0.06);
  vec3 spec = ggx(NoH, a) * smith(NoV, NoL, a) * F;
  float wrap = clamp((dot(n, L) + 0.3) / 1.3, 0.0, 1.0);
  col += (diffuseColor * (1.0 - F) / PI * wrap + spec * NoL) * radiance;
  vec3 sh = normalize(L + n * 0.1);
  float scatter = pow(clamp(dot(v, -sh), 0.0, 1.0), 2.0) * 10.0 + 0.04;
  col += scatter * albedo * radiance * 0.03 * uExtra.x;
  float alpha = 1.0;
  if (uExtra.y > 0.5) {
    float rim = pow(1.0 - NoV, 2.5);
    col += albedo * (studio(-R, 1.0) * 0.35 + 0.06) * (1.0 - rim);
    col += radiance * albedo * pow(max(dot(-v, L), 0.0), 3.0) * 0.08;
    alpha = mix(0.5, 0.96, rim);
  }
  if (coat > 0.0) {
    float Fc = 0.04 + 0.96 * pow(1.0 - NoV, 5.0);
    float ca = max(coatRough * coatRough, 0.03);
    vec3 coatEnv = studio(R, coatRough) * Fc;
    float coatSpec = ggx(NoH, ca) * smith(NoV, NoL, ca) * (0.04 + 0.96 * pow(1.0 - VoH, 5.0)) * NoL;
    col = col * (1.0 - Fc * coat) + (coatEnv * specAo + radiance * coatSpec) * coat;
    alpha = mix(alpha, 1.0, Fc * coat);
  }
  if (lamp) col += uLight * 0.0025 * (0.4 + 0.6 * albedo);
  col = aces(col * uExposure);
  col = pow(col, vec3(1.0 / 2.2));
  col += (fract(sin(dot(gl_FragCoord.xy, vec2(12.9898, 78.233))) * 43758.5453) - 0.5) / 255.0;
  float a2 = alpha * cover;
  outColor = vec4(col * a2, a2);
}`;

const clamp = (value, min, max) => Math.min(max, Math.max(min, value));

const compile = (gl, type, source) => {
  const shader = gl.createShader(type);
  if (!shader) return null;
  gl.shaderSource(shader, source);
  gl.compileShader(shader);
  if (gl.getShaderParameter(shader, gl.COMPILE_STATUS)) return shader;
  gl.deleteShader(shader);
  return null;
};

const link = gl => {
  const vertex = compile(gl, gl.VERTEX_SHADER, VERTEX);
  const fragment = compile(gl, gl.FRAGMENT_SHADER, FRAGMENT);
  const program = gl.createProgram();
  if (!vertex || !fragment || !program) return null;
  gl.attachShader(program, vertex);
  gl.attachShader(program, fragment);
  gl.linkProgram(program);
  gl.deleteShader(vertex);
  gl.deleteShader(fragment);
  if (!gl.getProgramParameter(program, gl.LINK_STATUS)) {
    gl.deleteProgram(program);
    return null;
  }
  const uniforms = {};
  const total = gl.getProgramParameter(program, gl.ACTIVE_UNIFORMS);
  for (let i = 0; i < total; i++) {
    const info = gl.getActiveUniform(program, i);
    if (info) uniforms[info.name] = gl.getUniformLocation(program, info.name);
  }
  return { program, uniforms };
};

const toLinear = value => {
  let rgb;
  if (typeof value === 'number') {
    rgb = [((value >> 16) & 255) / 255, ((value >> 8) & 255) / 255, (value & 255) / 255];
  } else {
    let hex = String(value || '#ffffff')
      .replace('#', '')
      .trim();
    if (hex.length === 3 || hex.length === 4)
      hex = hex
        .slice(0, 3)
        .split('')
        .map(c => c + c)
        .join('');
    const num = parseInt(hex.slice(0, 6), 16);
    rgb = Number.isNaN(num) ? [1, 1, 1] : [((num >> 16) & 255) / 255, ((num >> 8) & 255) / 255, (num & 255) / 255];
  }
  return rgb.map(c => Math.pow(c, 2.2));
};

const Ballpit = ({
  count = 200,
  colors = ['#ffffff'],
  material = 'satin',
  minSize = 0.5,
  maxSize = 1,
  gravity = 0.5,
  friction = 0.9975,
  wallBounce = 0.95,
  bounce = 0.85,
  depth = 2,
  drift = 0,
  followCursor = true,
  cursorSize = 1,
  grab = true,
  clickEffect = 'burst',
  lightColor = '#ffffff',
  lightIntensity = 1,
  exposure = 1,
  shadows = 0.6,
  intro = true,
  dpr,
  className = '',
  style
}) => {
  const containerRef = useRef(null);
  const canvasRef = useRef(null);
  const wakeRef = useRef(null);
  const releaseRef = useRef(0);
  const settings = {
    count: Math.round(clamp(count, 1, 4000)),
    colors: Array.isArray(colors) && colors.length ? colors : ['#ffffff'],
    material: MATERIALS[material] ? material : 'satin',
    minSize: Math.max(0.05, Math.min(minSize, maxSize)),
    maxSize: Math.max(0.05, Math.max(minSize, maxSize)),
    gravity,
    friction: clamp(friction, 0.5, 1),
    wallBounce: clamp(wallBounce, 0, 1),
    bounce: clamp(bounce, 0, 1),
    depth: Math.max(0, depth),
    drift: Math.max(0, drift),
    followCursor,
    cursorSize: Math.max(0, cursorSize),
    grab,
    clickEffect,
    lightColor,
    lightIntensity: lightIntensity > 20 ? lightIntensity / 200 : Math.max(0, lightIntensity),
    exposure: Math.max(0, exposure),
    shadows: clamp(shadows, 0, 1),
    intro,
    dpr
  };
  const settingsRef = useRef(settings);
  settingsRef.current = settings;

  useEffect(() => {
    window.clearTimeout(releaseRef.current);
    const container = containerRef.current;
    const canvas = canvasRef.current;
    const gl = canvas?.getContext('webgl2', {
      alpha: true,
      premultipliedAlpha: true,
      antialias: false,
      depth: true,
      stencil: false,
      powerPreference: 'high-performance'
    });
    if (!container || !canvas || !gl) return undefined;
    const shader = link(gl);
    if (!shader) return undefined;
    const u = shader.uniforms;

    const reduce = window.matchMedia?.('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches ?? false;
    const view = { width: 1, height: 1, ratio: 1, halfW: 9, halfH: 9, fit: 1, tan: Math.tan((FOV * Math.PI) / 360) };
    const projection = new Float32Array(16);

    const vao = gl.createVertexArray();
    gl.bindVertexArray(vao);
    const quad = gl.createBuffer();
    gl.bindBuffer(gl.ARRAY_BUFFER, quad);
    gl.bufferData(gl.ARRAY_BUFFER, new Float32Array([-1, -1, 1, -1, -1, 1, 1, 1]), gl.STATIC_DRAW);
    gl.enableVertexAttribArray(0);
    gl.vertexAttribPointer(0, 2, gl.FLOAT, false, 0, 0);
    const instances = gl.createBuffer();
    gl.bindBuffer(gl.ARRAY_BUFFER, instances);
    for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) {
      gl.enableVertexAttribArray(1 + k);
      gl.vertexAttribPointer(1 + k, 4, gl.FLOAT, false, STRIDE * 4, k * 16);
      gl.vertexAttribDivisor(1 + k, 1);
    }
    gl.bindVertexArray(null);

    let capacity = 0;
    let n = 0;
    let px = new Float32Array(0);
    let py = new Float32Array(0);
    let pz = new Float32Array(0);
    let vx = new Float32Array(0);
    let vy = new Float32Array(0);
    let vz = new Float32Array(0);
    let rad = new Float32Array(0);
    let inv = new Float32Array(0);
    let size = new Float32Array(0);
    let phase = new Float32Array(0);
    let occ = new Float32Array(0);
    let still = new Float32Array(0);
    let tone = new Uint16Array(0);
    let entered = new Uint8Array(0);
    let cellOf = new Int32Array(0);
    let sorted = new Int32Array(0);
    let order = new Int32Array(1);
    let data = new Float32Array(STRIDE);
    let table = new Int32Array(2);
    let starts = new Int32Array(2);
    let palette = [];
    let paletteKey = '';
    let lastSizes = '';

    const allocate = total => {
      const grow = (old, width = 1) => {
        const next = new Float32Array(total * width);
        next.set(old.subarray(0, Math.min(old.length, next.length)));
        return next;
      };
      const growUint8 = old => {
        const next = new Uint8Array(total);
        next.set(old.subarray(0, Math.min(old.length, next.length)));
        return next;
      };
      px = grow(px);
      py = grow(py);
      pz = grow(pz);
      vx = grow(vx);
      vy = grow(vy);
      vz = grow(vz);
      rad = grow(rad);
      inv = grow(inv);
      size = grow(size);
      phase = grow(phase);
      occ = grow(occ, 4);
      still = grow(still, 3);
      const nextTone = new Uint16Array(total);
      nextTone.set(tone.subarray(0, Math.min(tone.length, total)));
      tone = nextTone;
      entered = growUint8(entered);
      cellOf = new Int32Array(total);
      sorted = new Int32Array(total);
      order = new Int32Array(total + 1);
      data = new Float32Array((total + 1) * STRIDE);
      capacity = total;
      gl.bindBuffer(gl.ARRAY_BUFFER, instances);
      gl.bufferData(gl.ARRAY_BUFFER, data.byteLength, gl.DYNAMIC_DRAW);
    };

    const pointer = { x: 0, y: 0, inside: false, down: false, downX: 0, downY: 0, downAt: 0, moved: false };
    const cursor = { x: 0, y: 0, z: 0, r: 0, tx: 0, ty: 0, vx: 0, vy: 0 };
    const light = { x: 0, y: 5, z: 6, flash: 0 };
    const held = { index: -1, ox: 0, oy: 0, tx: 0, ty: 0, vx: 0, vy: 0 };
    let elapsed = 0;
    let glide = 0;
    let raf = 0;
    let last = 0;
    let visible = true;
    let quiet = 0;

    const spawn = (i, s, fromTop) => {
      const r = rad[i];
      const zr = Math.max(s.depth - r, 0);
      entered[i] = 1;
      if (fromTop && s.gravity > 0.05) {
        px[i] = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * Math.max(view.halfW - r, 0.01);
        py[i] = view.halfH + r + Math.random() * view.halfH * 2.2;
        pz[i] = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * zr;
        vx[i] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 2;
        vy[i] = -(4 + Math.random() * 5);
        vz[i] = 0;
        entered[i] = 0;
      } else if (fromTop) {
        const tx = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * Math.max(view.halfW - r, 0.01);
        const ty = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * Math.max(view.halfH - r, 0.01);
        const tz = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * zr;
        px[i] = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * 0.6;
        py[i] = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * 0.6;
        pz[i] = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * Math.min(zr, 0.3);
        vx[i] = (tx - px[i]) * 2.2;
        vy[i] = (ty - py[i]) * 2.2;
        vz[i] = (tz - pz[i]) * 2.2;
        glide = 1;
      } else {
        px[i] = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * Math.max(view.halfW - r, 0.01);
        py[i] = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * Math.max(view.halfH - r, 0.01);
        pz[i] = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * zr;
        vx[i] = 0;
        vy[i] = 0;
        vz[i] = 0;
      }
    };

    const applySizes = s => {
      for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        rad[i] = (s.minSize + size[i] * (s.maxSize - s.minSize)) * view.fit;
        inv[i] = 1 / (rad[i] * rad[i] * rad[i]);
      }
    };

    const applyPalette = s => {
      palette = s.colors.map(toLinear);
      paletteKey = s.colors.join('|');
    };

    const sync = (s, first) => {
      const key = `${s.minSize}:${s.maxSize}:${view.fit}`;
      if (s.count !== n) {
        if (s.count > capacity) allocate(Math.max(s.count, Math.ceil(capacity * 1.5)));
        const from = n;
        n = s.count;
        for (let i = from; i < n; i++) {
          size[i] = Math.random();
          tone[i] = i;
          phase[i] = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
          rad[i] = (s.minSize + size[i] * (s.maxSize - s.minSize)) * view.fit;
          inv[i] = 1 / (rad[i] * rad[i] * rad[i]);
          spawn(i, s, (first ? s.intro : true) && !reduce);
        }
        for (let i = 0; i <= n; i++) order[i] = i;
      }
      if (key !== lastSizes) {
        lastSizes = key;
        applySizes(s);
      }
      if (s.colors.join('|') !== paletteKey) applyPalette(s);
    };

    let contactLimit = 0;
    let contactCount = 0;
    let pairs = new Int32Array(0);
    let normals = new Float32Array(0);
    let targets = new Float64Array(0);
    let impulses = new Float32Array(0);

    const grid = { scale: 1, x0: 0, y0: 0, z0: 0, nx: 1, ny: 1, nz: 1 };

    const buildGrid = (cell, s) => {
      grid.scale = 1 / cell;
      grid.x0 = -view.halfW - cell;
      grid.y0 = -view.halfH - cell;
      grid.z0 = -Math.max(s.depth, s.maxSize) - cell;
      grid.nx = Math.max(1, Math.ceil((view.halfW * 2 + cell * 2) * grid.scale) + 1);
      grid.ny = Math.max(1, Math.ceil((view.halfH * 2 + cell * 2) * grid.scale) + 1);
      grid.nz = Math.max(1, Math.ceil((Math.max(s.depth, s.maxSize) * 2 + cell * 2) * grid.scale) + 1);
      const cells = grid.nx * grid.ny * grid.nz;
      if (table.length < cells + 1) {
        table = new Int32Array(cells + 1);
        starts = new Int32Array(cells + 1);
      }
      table.fill(0, 0, cells + 1);
      for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        const cx = clamp(Math.floor((px[i] - grid.x0) * grid.scale), 0, grid.nx - 1);
        const cy = clamp(Math.floor((py[i] - grid.y0) * grid.scale), 0, grid.ny - 1);
        const cz = clamp(Math.floor((pz[i] - grid.z0) * grid.scale), 0, grid.nz - 1);
        const c = (cz * grid.ny + cy) * grid.nx + cx;
        cellOf[i] = c;
        table[c + 1]++;
      }
      for (let c = 1; c <= cells; c++) table[c] += table[c - 1];
      starts.set(table.subarray(0, cells + 1));
      for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) sorted[starts[cellOf[i]]++] = i;
    };

    const wall = (i, gap, nx, ny, nz, bounce, margin, dt) => {
      if (gap >= margin || contactCount >= contactLimit) return;
      const approach = vx[i] * nx + vy[i] * ny + vz[i] * nz;
      const c = contactCount;
      const near = Math.fround(gap);
      pairs[c * 2] = i;
      pairs[c * 2 + 1] = -2;
      normals[c * 3] = nx;
      normals[c * 3 + 1] = ny;
      normals[c * 3 + 2] = nz;
      targets[c] = near > 0 ? -near / dt : Math.fround(approach < -1.5 ? -bounce * approach : 0);
      impulses[c] = 0;
      contactCount++;
    };

    const findContacts = (s, dt, shade, margin) => {
      const heldIndex = held.index;
      const { nx: gx, ny: gy, nz: gz } = grid;
      const X = px;
      const Y = py;
      const Z = pz;
      const VX = vx;
      const VY = vy;
      const VZ = vz;
      const R = rad;
      const shadeOf = occ;
      const cells = cellOf;
      const firsts = table;
      const members = sorted;
      const ends = pairs;
      const axes = normals;
      const goals = targets;
      const sums = impulses;
      const limit = contactLimit;
      const bounce = s.bounce;
      contactCount = 0;
      for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        const c = cells[i];
        const cx = c % gx;
        const cy = Math.floor(c / gx) % gy;
        const cz = Math.floor(c / (gx * gy));
        const xi = X[i];
        const yi = Y[i];
        const zi = Z[i];
        const ri = R[i];
        const left = cx > 0 ? cx - 1 : cx;
        const right = cx < gx - 1 ? cx + 2 : cx + 1;
        for (let dz = cz > 0 ? -1 : 0; dz <= (cz < gz - 1 ? 1 : 0); dz++) {
          for (let dy = cy > 0 ? -1 : 0; dy <= (cy < gy - 1 ? 1 : 0); dy++) {
            const row = ((cz + dz) * gy + cy + dy) * gx;
            const end = firsts[row + right];
            for (let k = firsts[row + left]; k < end; k++) {
              const j = members[k];
              if (j <= i) continue;
              const ddx = X[j] - xi;
              const ddy = Y[j] - yi;
              const ddz = Z[j] - zi;
              const d2 = ddx * ddx + ddy * ddy + ddz * ddz;
              const sum = ri + R[j];
              const reach = Math.max(sum + margin, shade ? sum * 1.4 : 0);
              if (d2 >= reach * reach || d2 < 1e-12) continue;
              const d = Math.sqrt(d2);
              const nx = ddx / d;
              const ny = ddy / d;
              const nz = ddz / d;
              if (shade) {
                const w = clamp(1 - (d - sum) / (sum * 0.4), 0, 1);
                shadeOf[i * 4] += nx * w;
                shadeOf[i * 4 + 1] += ny * w;
                shadeOf[i * 4 + 2] += nz * w;
                shadeOf[i * 4 + 3] += w;
                shadeOf[j * 4] -= nx * w;
                shadeOf[j * 4 + 1] -= ny * w;
                shadeOf[j * 4 + 2] -= nz * w;
                shadeOf[j * 4 + 3] += w;
              }
              if (d >= sum + margin || contactCount >= limit) continue;
              if (i === heldIndex && j === heldIndex) continue;
              const approach = (VX[j] - VX[i]) * nx + (VY[j] - VY[i]) * ny + (VZ[j] - VZ[i]) * nz;
              const c = contactCount;
              const near = Math.fround(d - sum);
              ends[c * 2] = i;
              ends[c * 2 + 1] = j;
              axes[c * 3] = nx;
              axes[c * 3 + 1] = ny;
              axes[c * 3 + 2] = nz;
              goals[c] = near > 0 ? -near / dt : Math.fround(approach < -1.5 ? -bounce * approach : 0);
              sums[c] = 0;
              contactCount++;
            }
          }
        }
        if (i !== heldIndex) {
          const r = ri;
          const zr = Math.max(s.depth - r, 0);
          wall(i, xi + view.halfW - r, 1, 0, 0, s.wallBounce, margin, dt);
          wall(i, view.halfW - r - xi, -1, 0, 0, s.wallBounce, margin, dt);
          wall(i, yi + view.halfH - r, 0, 1, 0, s.wallBounce, margin, dt);
          if (entered[i]) wall(i, view.halfH - r - yi, 0, -1, 0, s.wallBounce, margin, dt);
          wall(i, zi + zr, 0, 0, 1, s.wallBounce, margin, dt);
          wall(i, zr - zi, 0, 0, -1, s.wallBounce, margin, dt);
        }
        if (cursor.r > 0.01 && i !== heldIndex && contactCount < limit) {
          const ddx = xi - cursor.x;
          const ddy = yi - cursor.y;
          const ddz = zi - cursor.z;
          const sum = ri + cursor.r;
          const d2 = ddx * ddx + ddy * ddy + ddz * ddz;
          if (d2 < (sum + margin) * (sum + margin) && d2 > 1e-12) {
            const d = Math.sqrt(d2);
            const nx = ddx / d;
            const ny = ddy / d;
            const nz = ddz / d;
            const approach = (VX[i] - cursor.vx) * nx + (VY[i] - cursor.vy) * ny + VZ[i] * nz;
            const c = contactCount;
            const near = Math.fround(d - sum);
            ends[c * 2] = i;
            ends[c * 2 + 1] = -1;
            axes[c * 3] = nx;
            axes[c * 3 + 1] = ny;
            axes[c * 3 + 2] = nz;
            goals[c] = near > 0 ? -near / dt : Math.fround(approach < -1.5 ? -bounce * approach : 0);
            sums[c] = 0;
            contactCount++;
          }
        }
      }
    };

    const solveVelocities = () => {
      const heldIndex = held.index;
      const iterations = n > 800 ? 5 : 8;
      const VX = vx;
      const VY = vy;
      const VZ = vz;
      const weights = inv;
      const ends = pairs;
      const axes = normals;
      const goals = targets;
      const sums = impulses;
      const total = contactCount;
      const cursorVx = cursor.vx;
      const cursorVy = cursor.vy;
      for (let iteration = 0; iteration < iterations; iteration++) {
        for (let c = 0; c < total; c++) {
          const i = ends[c * 2];
          const j = ends[c * 2 + 1];
          const nx = axes[c * 3];
          const ny = axes[c * 3 + 1];
          const nz = axes[c * 3 + 2];
          const target = goals[c];
          const before = sums[c];
          if (j < 0) {
            const vn =
              j === -1
                ? (VX[i] - cursorVx) * nx + (VY[i] - cursorVy) * ny + VZ[i] * nz
                : VX[i] * nx + VY[i] * ny + VZ[i] * nz;
            const after = Math.max(before + target - vn, 0);
            const change = after - before;
            sums[c] = after;
            VX[i] += change * nx;
            VY[i] += change * ny;
            VZ[i] += change * nz;
            continue;
          }
          const wi = i === heldIndex ? 0 : weights[i];
          const wj = j === heldIndex ? 0 : weights[j];
          const w = wi + wj;
          if (w <= 0) continue;
          const vn = (VX[j] - VX[i]) * nx + (VY[j] - VY[i]) * ny + (VZ[j] - VZ[i]) * nz;
          const after = Math.max(before + (target - vn) / w, 0);
          const change = after - before;
          sums[c] = after;
          VX[i] -= change * wi * nx;
          VY[i] -= change * wi * ny;
          VZ[i] -= change * wi * nz;
          VX[j] += change * wj * nx;
          VY[j] += change * wj * ny;
          VZ[j] += change * wj * nz;
        }
      }
    };

    const correctPositions = () => {
      const heldIndex = held.index;
      const X = px;
      const Y = py;
      const Z = pz;
      const R = rad;
      const weights = inv;
      const ends = pairs;
      const total = contactCount;
      const cursorX = cursor.x;
      const cursorY = cursor.y;
      const cursorZ = cursor.z;
      const cursorR = cursor.r;
      for (let pass = 0; pass < 2; pass++) {
        for (let c = 0; c < total; c++) {
          const i = ends[c * 2];
          const j = ends[c * 2 + 1];
          if (j === -2) continue;
          if (j < 0) {
            const ddx = X[i] - cursorX;
            const ddy = Y[i] - cursorY;
            const ddz = Z[i] - cursorZ;
            const d = Math.sqrt(ddx * ddx + ddy * ddy + ddz * ddz);
            const overlap = R[i] + cursorR - d - SLOP;
            if (overlap <= 0 || d < 1e-6) continue;
            const k = (overlap * 0.5) / d;
            X[i] += ddx * k;
            Y[i] += ddy * k;
            Z[i] += ddz * k;
            continue;
          }
          const ddx = X[j] - X[i];
          const ddy = Y[j] - Y[i];
          const ddz = Z[j] - Z[i];
          const d = Math.sqrt(ddx * ddx + ddy * ddy + ddz * ddz);
          const overlap = R[i] + R[j] - d - SLOP;
          if (overlap <= 0 || d < 1e-6) continue;
          const wi = i === heldIndex ? 0 : weights[i];
          const wj = j === heldIndex ? 0 : weights[j];
          const w = wi + wj;
          if (w <= 0) continue;
          const k = (overlap * 0.3) / (d * w);
          X[i] -= ddx * k * wi;
          Y[i] -= ddy * k * wi;
          Z[i] -= ddz * k * wi;
          X[j] += ddx * k * wj;
          Y[j] += ddy * k * wj;
          Z[j] += ddz * k * wj;
        }
      }
    };

    const walls = s => {
      const hw = view.halfW;
      const hh = view.halfH;
      const e = s.wallBounce;
      for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        const r = rad[i];
        if (px[i] < -hw + r) {
          px[i] = -hw + r;
          if (vx[i] < 0) vx[i] = vx[i] < -1.5 ? -vx[i] * e : 0;
        } else if (px[i] > hw - r) {
          px[i] = hw - r;
          if (vx[i] > 0) vx[i] = vx[i] > 1.5 ? -vx[i] * e : 0;
        }
        if (py[i] < -hh + r) {
          py[i] = -hh + r;
          if (vy[i] < 0) vy[i] = vy[i] < -1.5 ? -vy[i] * e : 0;
          vx[i] *= 0.995;
          vz[i] *= 0.995;
        } else if (entered[i] && py[i] > hh - r) {
          py[i] = hh - r;
          if (vy[i] > 0) vy[i] = vy[i] > 1.5 ? -vy[i] * e : 0;
        }
        if (!entered[i] && py[i] < hh - r) entered[i] = 1;
        const zr = Math.max(s.depth - r, 0);
        if (pz[i] < -zr) {
          pz[i] = -zr;
          if (vz[i] < 0) vz[i] = vz[i] < -1.5 ? -vz[i] * e : 0;
        } else if (pz[i] > zr) {
          pz[i] = zr;
          if (vz[i] > 0) vz[i] = vz[i] > 1.5 ? -vz[i] * e : 0;
        }
      }
    };

    const step = (s, dt, shade) => {
      const g = s.gravity * 45;
      const damp = Math.pow(s.friction, dt * 60) * (glide > 0 ? Math.exp(-2.2 * dt) : 1);
      const wander = reduce ? 0 : s.drift * 2.2;
      const biggest = s.maxSize * view.fit;
      const margin = biggest * 0.3;
      if (contactLimit < n * 12) {
        contactLimit = n * 12;
        pairs = new Int32Array(contactLimit * 2);
        normals = new Float32Array(contactLimit * 3);
        targets = new Float64Array(contactLimit);
        impulses = new Float32Array(contactLimit);
      }
      for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        if (i === held.index) {
          const f = 1 - Math.exp(-dt / 0.02);
          vx[i] = ((held.tx - px[i]) * f) / dt;
          vy[i] = ((held.ty - py[i]) * f) / dt;
          vz[i] = 0;
          continue;
        }
        vy[i] -= g * dt;
        if (wander) {
          const p = phase[i];
          vx[i] += Math.sin(elapsed * 0.63 + p) * wander * dt;
          vy[i] += Math.cos(elapsed * 0.47 + p * 1.7) * wander * dt;
          vz[i] += Math.sin(elapsed * 0.41 + p * 2.3) * wander * dt * 0.5;
        }
        vx[i] *= damp;
        vy[i] *= damp;
        vz[i] *= damp;
        const sp = vx[i] * vx[i] + vy[i] * vy[i] + vz[i] * vz[i];
        if (sp > MAX_SPEED * MAX_SPEED) {
          const f = MAX_SPEED / Math.sqrt(sp);
          vx[i] *= f;
          vy[i] *= f;
          vz[i] *= f;
        }
      }
      buildGrid(biggest * (shade ? 2.8 : 2.3), s);
      if (shade) occ.fill(0, 0, n * 4);
      findContacts(s, dt, shade, margin);
      solveVelocities();
      for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        px[i] += vx[i] * dt;
        py[i] += vy[i] * dt;
        pz[i] += vz[i] * dt;
      }
      correctPositions();
      walls(s);
      if (shade) {
        for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
          const gap = py[i] - rad[i] + view.halfH;
          const reach = rad[i] * 0.5;
          if (gap >= reach) continue;
          const w = 1 - Math.max(gap, 0) / reach;
          occ[i * 4 + 1] -= w;
          occ[i * 4 + 3] += w;
        }
      }
      if (held.index >= 0) {
        const i = held.index;
        held.vx += (vx[i] - held.vx) * (1 - Math.exp(-dt / 0.05));
        held.vy += (vy[i] - held.vy) * (1 - Math.exp(-dt / 0.05));
      }
    };

    const rayTo = (clientX, clientY) => {
      const rect = container.getBoundingClientRect();
      const nx = ((clientX - rect.left) / Math.max(rect.width, 1)) * 2 - 1;
      const ny = 1 - ((clientY - rect.top) / Math.max(rect.height, 1)) * 2;
      return [nx * view.tan * (view.width / view.height), ny * view.tan, -1];
    };

    const onPlane = (ray, z) => {
      const t = (z - CAMERA_Z) / ray[2];
      return [ray[0] * t, ray[1] * t];
    };

    const pick = ray => {
      const len = Math.hypot(ray[0], ray[1], ray[2]);
      const dx = ray[0] / len;
      const dy = ray[1] / len;
      const dz = ray[2] / len;
      let best = -1;
      let bestT = Infinity;
      for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        const ox = -px[i];
        const oy = -py[i];
        const oz = CAMERA_Z - pz[i];
        const b = ox * dx + oy * dy + oz * dz;
        const r = rad[i] * 1.15;
        const c = ox * ox + oy * oy + oz * oz - r * r;
        const disc = b * b - c;
        if (disc < 0) continue;
        const t = -b - Math.sqrt(disc);
        if (t > 0 && t < bestT) {
          bestT = t;
          best = i;
        }
      }
      return best;
    };

    const burst = (x, y) => {
      const s = settingsRef.current;
      const reach = view.halfH * 0.7;
      for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        const dx = px[i] - x;
        const dy = py[i] - y;
        const dz = pz[i];
        const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy, dz);
        if (d >= reach || d < 1e-4) continue;
        const f = Math.pow(1 - d / reach, 2) * 26 * (1 + 0.6 / (rad[i] + 0.4));
        vx[i] += (dx / d) * f;
        vy[i] += (dy / d) * f + f * 0.25;
        vz[i] += (dz / d) * f * 0.5;
      }
      light.x = x;
      light.y = y;
      light.z = Math.max(s.depth, 1) + 1.5;
      light.flash = 1;
    };

    let sizedDpr = settingsRef.current.dpr;

    const resize = () => {
      const s = settingsRef.current;
      sizedDpr = s.dpr;
      view.width = Math.max(1, container.clientWidth);
      view.height = Math.max(1, container.clientHeight);
      view.ratio = Math.min(s.dpr || window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2);
      canvas.width = Math.max(1, Math.round(view.width * view.ratio));
      canvas.height = Math.max(1, Math.round(view.height * view.ratio));
      const aspect = view.width / view.height;
      const base = Math.tan((FOV * Math.PI) / 360);
      view.tan = aspect > MAX_ASPECT ? (base * MAX_ASPECT) / aspect : base;
      view.halfH = view.tan * CAMERA_Z;
      view.halfW = view.halfH * aspect;
      view.fit = clamp(Math.sqrt((view.halfW * view.halfH) / (base * base * CAMERA_Z * CAMERA_Z * MAX_ASPECT)), 0.4, 1);
      const f = 1 / view.tan;
      const near = 1;
      const far = 80;
      projection.fill(0);
      projection[0] = f / aspect;
      projection[5] = f;
      projection[10] = (far + near) / (near - far);
      projection[11] = -1;
      projection[14] = (2 * far * near) / (near - far);
      if (!n) sync(s, true);
      draw();
      wake();
    };

    const draw = () => {
      const s = settingsRef.current;
      const mat = MATERIALS[s.material];
      let count = 0;
      const total = n + 1;
      for (let a = 1; a < total; a++) {
        const item = order[a];
        const z = item === n ? cursor.z : pz[item];
        let b = a - 1;
        while (b >= 0 && (order[b] === n ? cursor.z : pz[order[b]]) > z) {
          order[b + 1] = order[b];
          b--;
        }
        order[b + 1] = item;
      }
      for (let k = 0; k < total; k++) {
        const i = order[k];
        const o = count * STRIDE;
        if (i === n) {
          if (cursor.r < 0.01 || !s.followCursor) continue;
          const c = palette[0] || [1, 1, 1];
          data[o] = cursor.x;
          data[o + 1] = cursor.y;
          data[o + 2] = cursor.z;
          data[o + 3] = cursor.r;
          data[o + 4] = c[0];
          data[o + 5] = c[1];
          data[o + 6] = c[2];
          data[o + 7] = 1;
          data[o + 8] = 0;
          data[o + 9] = 0;
          data[o + 10] = 0;
          data[o + 11] = 0;
        } else {
          const c = palette[tone[i] % palette.length];
          data[o] = px[i];
          data[o + 1] = py[i];
          data[o + 2] = pz[i];
          data[o + 3] = rad[i];
          data[o + 4] = c[0];
          data[o + 5] = c[1];
          data[o + 6] = c[2];
          data[o + 7] = 0;
          const w = occ[i * 4 + 3];
          const len = Math.hypot(occ[i * 4], occ[i * 4 + 1], occ[i * 4 + 2]) || 1;
          data[o + 8] = occ[i * 4] / len;
          data[o + 9] = occ[i * 4 + 1] / len;
          data[o + 10] = occ[i * 4 + 2] / len;
          data[o + 11] = Math.min(w * 0.55, 1);
        }
        count++;
      }
      gl.viewport(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
      gl.clearColor(0, 0, 0, 0);
      gl.clearDepth(1);
      gl.clear(gl.COLOR_BUFFER_BIT | gl.DEPTH_BUFFER_BIT);
      if (!count) return;
      gl.enable(gl.DEPTH_TEST);
      gl.depthFunc(gl.LEQUAL);
      gl.enable(gl.BLEND);
      gl.blendFunc(gl.ONE, gl.ONE_MINUS_SRC_ALPHA);
      gl.useProgram(shader.program);
      gl.uniformMatrix4fv(u.uProj, false, projection);
      gl.uniform1f(u.uCameraZ, CAMERA_Z);
      gl.uniform1f(u.uPixel, (2 * view.tan) / canvas.height);
      const lc = toLinear(s.lightColor);
      const power = s.lightIntensity * 60 * (1 + light.flash * 3);
      gl.uniform3f(u.uLight, lc[0] * power, lc[1] * power, lc[2] * power);
      gl.uniform3f(u.uLightPos, light.x, light.y, light.z - CAMERA_Z);
      gl.uniform1f(u.uExposure, s.exposure);
      gl.uniform1f(u.uShadow, s.shadows);
      gl.uniform4f(u.uSurface, mat.roughness, mat.metalness, mat.coat, mat.coatRoughness);
      gl.uniform2f(u.uExtra, mat.scatter, mat.frost);
      gl.bindBuffer(gl.ARRAY_BUFFER, instances);
      gl.bufferSubData(gl.ARRAY_BUFFER, 0, data, 0, count * STRIDE);
      gl.bindVertexArray(vao);
      gl.drawArraysInstanced(gl.TRIANGLE_STRIP, 0, 4, count);
      gl.bindVertexArray(null);
    };

    const frame = now => {
      raf = 0;
      if (!visible || document.hidden) return;
      const s = settingsRef.current;
      if (s.dpr !== sizedDpr) resize();
      const dt = last ? clamp((now - last) / 1000, 0, 1 / 20) : 1 / 60;
      last = now;
      elapsed += dt;
      glide = Math.max(0, glide - dt);
      sync(s, false);
      const targetR = s.followCursor && pointer.inside && held.index < 0 ? s.cursorSize * view.fit : 0;
      cursor.r += (targetR - cursor.r) * (1 - Math.exp(-dt / 0.12));
      const follow = 1 - Math.exp(-dt / 0.06);
      const fromX = cursor.x;
      const fromY = cursor.y;
      cursor.x += (cursor.tx - cursor.x) * follow;
      cursor.y += (cursor.ty - cursor.y) * follow;
      cursor.vx = clamp((cursor.x - fromX) / Math.max(dt, 1e-3), -MAX_SPEED, MAX_SPEED);
      cursor.vy = clamp((cursor.y - fromY) / Math.max(dt, 1e-3), -MAX_SPEED, MAX_SPEED);
      const restX = 0;
      const restY = view.halfH * 0.75;
      const restZ = s.depth + 4;
      const lightTarget = pointer.inside
        ? [cursor.x, cursor.y, s.followCursor ? cursor.z : 1.5]
        : light.flash > 0.05
          ? [light.x, light.y, light.z]
          : [restX, restY, restZ];
      const lf = 1 - Math.exp(-dt / 0.08);
      light.x += (lightTarget[0] - light.x) * lf;
      light.y += (lightTarget[1] - light.y) * lf;
      light.z += (lightTarget[2] - light.z) * lf;
      light.flash *= Math.exp(-dt / 0.3);
      if (dt > 1e-4) {
        const steps = Math.min(6, Math.max(1, Math.ceil(dt / (1 / 180))));
        const h = dt / steps;
        for (let k = 0; k < steps; k++) step(s, h, k === steps - 1);
      }
      draw();
      let moved = 0;
      for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        const k = i * 3;
        const d = Math.abs(px[i] - still[k]) + Math.abs(py[i] - still[k + 1]) + Math.abs(pz[i] - still[k + 2]);
        if (d > moved) moved = d;
        still[k] = px[i];
        still[k + 1] = py[i];
        still[k + 2] = pz[i];
      }
      const motion = moved / Math.max(dt, 1e-3);
      const busy =
        motion > 0.6 ||
        pointer.inside ||
        held.index >= 0 ||
        light.flash > 0.01 ||
        glide > 0 ||
        cursor.r > 0.01 ||
        (s.drift > 0 && !reduce);
      quiet = busy ? 0 : quiet + dt;
      if (quiet < 0.8) raf = requestAnimationFrame(frame);
    };

    function wake() {
      quiet = 0;
      if (!raf && visible && !document.hidden) {
        last = 0;
        raf = requestAnimationFrame(frame);
      }
    }

    const inside = event => {
      const rect = container.getBoundingClientRect();
      const x = event.clientX - rect.left;
      const y = event.clientY - rect.top;
      return x >= 0 && y >= 0 && x <= rect.width && y <= rect.height;
    };

    const aim = event => {
      const ray = rayTo(event.clientX, event.clientY);
      const [x, y] = onPlane(ray, cursor.z);
      cursor.tx = x;
      cursor.ty = y;
      if (held.index >= 0) {
        const [hx, hy] = onPlane(ray, pz[held.index]);
        held.tx = hx - held.ox;
        held.ty = hy - held.oy;
      }
      return ray;
    };

    const onPointerMove = event => {
      const was = pointer.inside;
      pointer.inside = inside(event) || held.index >= 0;
      if (pointer.inside) {
        aim(event);
        if (!was) {
          cursor.x = cursor.tx;
          cursor.y = cursor.ty;
        }
        if (pointer.down && Math.hypot(event.clientX - pointer.downX, event.clientY - pointer.downY) > 6)
          pointer.moved = true;
      }
      if (pointer.inside || was) wake();
    };

    const interactive = target =>
      target instanceof Element && target.closest('a, button, input, textarea, select, label, [role="button"]');

    const onPointerDown = event => {
      if (event.button !== 0 || !inside(event) || interactive(event.target)) return;
      const s = settingsRef.current;
      pointer.down = true;
      pointer.moved = false;
      pointer.downX = event.clientX;
      pointer.downY = event.clientY;
      pointer.downAt = performance.now();
      const ray = aim(event);
      if (s.grab) {
        const i = pick(ray);
        if (i >= 0) {
          const [hx, hy] = onPlane(ray, pz[i]);
          held.index = i;
          held.ox = hx - px[i];
          held.oy = hy - py[i];
          held.tx = px[i];
          held.ty = py[i];
          held.vx = 0;
          held.vy = 0;
          if (event.pointerType === 'mouse') event.preventDefault();
        }
      }
      wake();
    };

    const release = event => {
      const s = settingsRef.current;
      if (held.index >= 0) {
        const i = held.index;
        const sp = Math.hypot(held.vx, held.vy);
        const k = sp > 22 ? 22 / sp : 1;
        vx[i] = held.vx * k;
        vy[i] = held.vy * k;
        held.index = -1;
        entered[i] = 1;
      } else if (
        event &&
        pointer.down &&
        !pointer.moved &&
        performance.now() - pointer.downAt < 400 &&
        s.clickEffect === 'burst' &&
        !reduce &&
        inside(event)
      ) {
        const [x, y] = onPlane(rayTo(event.clientX, event.clientY), 0);
        burst(x, y);
      }
      pointer.down = false;
      wake();
    };

    const onPointerUp = event => release(event);
    const onPointerCancel = () => release(null);
    const onPointerLeave = () => {
      pointer.inside = held.index >= 0;
      wake();
    };
    const onVisibility = () => wake();

    const resizeObserver = new ResizeObserver(resize);
    resizeObserver.observe(container);
    const intersection = new IntersectionObserver(entries => {
      visible = entries.some(entry => entry.isIntersecting);
      if (visible) wake();
    });
    intersection.observe(container);
    window.addEventListener('pointermove', onPointerMove, { passive: true });
    window.addEventListener('pointerdown', onPointerDown);
    window.addEventListener('pointerup', onPointerUp, { passive: true });
    window.addEventListener('pointercancel', onPointerCancel, { passive: true });
    document.documentElement.addEventListener('pointerleave', onPointerLeave);
    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', onVisibility);
    resize();
    wakeRef.current = wake;

    return () => {
      cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
      wakeRef.current = null;
      resizeObserver.disconnect();
      intersection.disconnect();
      window.removeEventListener('pointermove', onPointerMove);
      window.removeEventListener('pointerdown', onPointerDown);
      window.removeEventListener('pointerup', onPointerUp);
      window.removeEventListener('pointercancel', onPointerCancel);
      document.documentElement.removeEventListener('pointerleave', onPointerLeave);
      document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', onVisibility);
      gl.deleteBuffer(quad);
      gl.deleteBuffer(instances);
      gl.deleteVertexArray(vao);
      gl.deleteProgram(shader.program);
      releaseRef.current = window.setTimeout(() => gl.getExtension('WEBGL_lose_context')?.loseContext(), 0);
    };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    wakeRef.current?.();
  });

  return (
    <div
      ref={containerRef}
      className={className}
      style={{ position: 'relative', width: '100%', height: '100%', overflow: 'hidden', ...style }}
    >
      <canvas ref={canvasRef} style={{ display: 'block', width: '100%', height: '100%' }} aria-hidden="true" />
    </div>
  );
};

export default Ballpit;
```

### Full Component Source
```jsx
'use client';

import { forwardRef, useEffect, useImperativeHandle, useRef } from 'react';

const EASINGS = {
  linear: t => t,
  'ease-in': t => t * t,
  'ease-out': t => 1 - Math.pow(1 - t, 3),
  'ease-in-out': t => (t < 0.5 ? 4 * t * t * t : 1 - Math.pow(-2 * t + 2, 3) / 2),
  spring: t => 1 + 2.4 * Math.pow(t - 1, 3) + 1.4 * Math.pow(t - 1, 2)
};

const SHAPES = ['line', 'streak', 'dot', 'star'];
const MAX_BURSTS = 32;
const GRAVITY = 500;
const REDUCED_MS = 320;

const clamp = (value, min, max) => Math.min(max, Math.max(min, value));
const smoothstep = (a, b, x) => {
  const t = clamp((x - a) / (b - a), 0, 1);
  return t * t * (3 - 2 * t);
};

const toColors = value => {
  const list = (Array.isArray(value) ? value : [value]).filter(Boolean);
  return list.length ? list : ['#ffffff'];
};

const starPath = (ctx, x, y, radius, rotation) => {
  const pinch = radius * 0.16;
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.moveTo(x + Math.cos(rotation) * radius, y + Math.sin(rotation) * radius);
  for (let i = 1; i <= 4; i++) {
    const a = rotation + (i * Math.PI) / 2;
    const mid = a - Math.PI / 4;
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(
      x + Math.cos(mid) * pinch,
      y + Math.sin(mid) * pinch,
      x + Math.cos(a) * radius,
      y + Math.sin(a) * radius
    );
  }
  ctx.closePath();
};

const channels = (ctx, color) => {
  ctx.fillStyle = color;
  const normal = String(ctx.fillStyle);
  const hex = /^#([0-9a-f]{6})$/i.exec(normal);
  if (hex) {
    const value = parseInt(hex[1], 16);
    return [(value >> 16) & 255, (value >> 8) & 255, value & 255];
  }
  const rgb = /^rgba?\(([^)]+)\)$/i.exec(normal);
  if (rgb)
    return rgb[1]
      .split(',')
      .slice(0, 3)
      .map(part => parseFloat(part));
  return [0, 0, 0];
};

const ClickSpark = forwardRef(
  (
    {
      sparkColor = '#fff',
      sparkSize = 14,
      sparkRadius = 30,
      sparkCount = 8,
      sparkWidth = 2,
      duration = 500,
      easing = 'ease-out',
      extraScale = 1,
      shape = 'line',
      randomness = 0.2,
      gravity = 0,
      spread = 360,
      direction = 0,
      glow = 0,
      ring = false,
      layer = 'front',
      disabled = false,
      className = '',
      style,
      children
    },
    ref
  ) => {
    const rootRef = useRef(null);
    const layerRef = useRef(null);
    const engineRef = useRef(null);
    const settings = {
      colors: toColors(sparkColor),
      size: Math.max(0, sparkSize),
      radius: Math.max(0, sparkRadius) * Math.max(0, extraScale),
      count: clamp(Math.round(sparkCount), 1, 64),
      width: Math.max(0.5, sparkWidth),
      duration: Math.max(60, duration),
      ease: EASINGS[easing] ?? EASINGS['ease-out'],
      shape: SHAPES.includes(shape) ? shape : 'line',
      randomness: clamp(randomness, 0, 1),
      gravity: Math.max(0, gravity),
      spread: clamp(spread, 0, 360),
      direction,
      glow: clamp(glow, 0, 1),
      ring,
      disabled
    };
    const settingsRef = useRef(settings);
    settingsRef.current = settings;

    useEffect(() => {
      const root = rootRef.current;
      const layer = layerRef.current;
      if (!root || !layer) return;

      const reduced = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
      const bursts = [];
      let raf = 0;
      let lastPress = { time: -1e9, x: 0, y: 0 };

      const drop = (s, seconds) => 0.5 * s.gravity * GRAVITY * seconds * seconds;

      const position = (burst, spark, distance, seconds) => [
        burst.x + Math.cos(spark.angle) * distance,
        burst.y + Math.sin(spark.angle) * distance + drop(burst.settings, seconds)
      ];

      const drawSpark = (ctx, burst, spark, elapsed) => {
        const s = burst.settings;
        const life = reduced ? REDUCED_MS : s.duration * spark.life;
        const p = elapsed / life;
        if (p >= 1) return false;
        const e = reduced ? 0.55 : s.ease(clamp(p, 0, 1));
        const seconds = reduced ? 0 : elapsed / 1000;
        const distance = s.radius * spark.reach * e;
        const width = s.width * spark.scale;
        const fade = reduced ? 1 - p : 1 - smoothstep(s.shape === 'line' ? 0.55 : 0.7, 1, p);
        if (fade <= 0) return true;
        ctx.strokeStyle = spark.color;
        ctx.fillStyle = spark.color;

        if (s.shape === 'line') {
          const length = s.size * spark.scale * (reduced ? 0.5 : Math.pow(1 - p, 1.6));
          const [x1, y1] = position(burst, spark, distance, seconds);
          const [x2, y2] = position(burst, spark, distance + Math.max(0.01, length), seconds);
          ctx.lineCap = 'round';
          if (spark.glow > 0) {
            ctx.globalAlpha = fade * 0.14 * spark.glow;
            ctx.lineWidth = width * 4.5;
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.moveTo(x1, y1);
            ctx.lineTo(x2, y2);
            ctx.stroke();
          }
          ctx.globalAlpha = fade;
          ctx.lineWidth = width;
          ctx.beginPath();
          ctx.moveTo(x1, y1);
          ctx.lineTo(x2, y2);
          ctx.stroke();
          return true;
        }

        if (s.shape === 'streak') {
          const lag = reduced ? 0 : Math.min(elapsed, 70 + s.size * 4 * spark.scale);
          const back = reduced ? 0.4 : s.ease(clamp((elapsed - lag) / life, 0, 1));
          const [hx, hy] = position(burst, spark, distance, seconds);
          const [tx, ty] = position(burst, spark, s.radius * spark.reach * back, Math.max(0, seconds - lag / 1000));
          const dx = hx - tx;
          const dy = hy - ty;
          const length = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
          const nx = (-dy / length) * width * 0.5;
          const ny = (dx / length) * width * 0.5;
          const paint = (spreadWidth, alpha) => {
            const gradient = ctx.createLinearGradient(tx, ty, hx, hy);
            gradient.addColorStop(0, spark.clear);
            gradient.addColorStop(1, spark.color);
            ctx.globalAlpha = alpha;
            ctx.fillStyle = gradient;
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.moveTo(tx, ty);
            ctx.lineTo(hx + nx * spreadWidth, hy + ny * spreadWidth);
            ctx.lineTo(hx - nx * spreadWidth, hy - ny * spreadWidth);
            ctx.closePath();
            ctx.fill();
            ctx.fillStyle = spark.color;
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.arc(hx, hy, width * 0.5 * spreadWidth, 0, Math.PI * 2);
            ctx.fill();
          };
          if (spark.glow > 0) paint(3.2, fade * 0.16 * spark.glow);
          paint(1, fade);
          return true;
        }

        if (s.shape === 'dot') {
          const [x, y] = position(burst, spark, distance, seconds);
          const radius = width * 1.25 * (1 - 0.5 * e);
          if (spark.glow > 0) {
            ctx.globalAlpha = fade * 0.18 * spark.glow;
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.arc(x, y, radius * 2.6, 0, Math.PI * 2);
            ctx.fill();
          }
          ctx.globalAlpha = fade;
          ctx.beginPath();
          ctx.arc(x, y, radius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
          ctx.fill();
          return true;
        }

        const [x, y] = position(burst, spark, distance, seconds);
        const twinkle = reduced ? 1 - p : Math.pow(Math.sin(Math.PI * clamp(p * 1.1, 0, 1)), 0.7);
        const radius = s.size * 0.6 * spark.scale * twinkle;
        const rotation = spark.angle + spark.spin * p * Math.PI * 0.5;
        if (radius <= 0.05) return true;
        if (spark.glow > 0) {
          ctx.globalAlpha = fade * 0.2 * spark.glow;
          ctx.beginPath();
          ctx.arc(x, y, radius * 1.1, 0, Math.PI * 2);
          ctx.fill();
        }
        ctx.globalAlpha = fade;
        starPath(ctx, x, y, radius, rotation);
        ctx.fill();
        return true;
      };

      const drawRing = (ctx, burst, elapsed) => {
        const s = burst.settings;
        const life = reduced ? REDUCED_MS : s.duration * 0.8;
        const p = elapsed / life;
        if (p >= 1) return false;
        const radius = 2 + s.radius * 0.6 * (reduced ? 0.6 : EASINGS['ease-out'](p));
        ctx.globalAlpha = Math.pow(1 - p, 2) * 0.7;
        ctx.strokeStyle = burst.sparks[0]?.color ?? s.colors[0];
        ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(0.5, s.width * (1 - p) * 0.9);
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(burst.x, burst.y, radius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.stroke();
        return true;
      };

      const remove = burst => {
        burst.canvas.remove();
        const index = bursts.indexOf(burst);
        if (index >= 0) bursts.splice(index, 1);
      };

      const frame = now => {
        raf = 0;
        for (const burst of [...bursts]) {
          const elapsed = now - burst.start;
          const ctx = burst.ctx;
          ctx.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);
          ctx.clearRect(0, 0, burst.canvas.width, burst.canvas.height);
          ctx.setTransform(burst.ratio, 0, 0, burst.ratio, -burst.left * burst.ratio, -burst.top * burst.ratio);
          let alive = false;
          if (burst.settings.ring && drawRing(ctx, burst, elapsed)) alive = true;
          for (const spark of burst.sparks) {
            if (drawSpark(ctx, burst, spark, elapsed)) alive = true;
          }
          ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
          if (!alive) remove(burst);
        }
        if (bursts.length) raf = requestAnimationFrame(frame);
      };

      const spawn = (clientX, clientY) => {
        const s = settingsRef.current;
        if (s.disabled) return;
        const rect = root.getBoundingClientRect();
        const offset = Math.floor(Math.random() * s.colors.length);
        const full = s.spread >= 359.5;
        const arc = (s.spread * Math.PI) / 180;
        const heading = ((s.direction - 90) * Math.PI) / 180;
        const step = full ? (Math.PI * 2) / s.count : s.count > 1 ? arc / (s.count - 1) : 0;
        const sparks = Array.from({ length: s.count }, (_, i) => {
          const base = full ? heading + step * i : s.count > 1 ? heading - arc / 2 + step * i : heading;
          const jitter = (Math.random() - 0.5) * s.randomness;
          return {
            angle: base + jitter * (step || arc || 1) * 0.9,
            reach: 1 + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.7 * s.randomness,
            scale: 1 + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.8 * s.randomness,
            life: 1 + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.5 * s.randomness,
            spin: Math.random() - 0.5,
            color: s.colors[(i + offset) % s.colors.length],
            clear: '',
            glow: 0
          };
        });
        const longest = s.duration * (1 + 0.25 * s.randomness);
        const fall = drop(s, longest / 1000);
        const reach =
          s.radius * (1 + 0.35 * s.randomness) * (s.ease === EASINGS.spring ? 1.15 : 1) +
          s.size * (1 + 0.4 * s.randomness) +
          s.width * (s.glow > 0 ? 6 : 2) +
          6;
        const box = {
          left: Math.max(clientX - reach, 0),
          top: Math.max(clientY - reach, 0),
          right: Math.min(clientX + reach, window.innerWidth),
          bottom: Math.min(clientY + reach + fall, window.innerHeight)
        };
        if (box.right - box.left < 1 || box.bottom - box.top < 1) return;
        const ratio = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2);
        const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
        canvas.width = Math.ceil((box.right - box.left) * ratio);
        canvas.height = Math.ceil((box.bottom - box.top) * ratio);
        Object.assign(canvas.style, {
          position: 'absolute',
          left: `${box.left - rect.left}px`,
          top: `${box.top - rect.top}px`,
          width: `${box.right - box.left}px`,
          height: `${box.bottom - box.top}px`,
          display: 'block',
          pointerEvents: 'none'
        });
        canvas.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
        layer.appendChild(canvas);
        const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
        if (!ctx) {
          canvas.remove();
          return;
        }
        for (const spark of sparks) {
          const [r, g, b] = channels(ctx, spark.color);
          spark.clear = `rgba(${r}, ${g}, ${b}, 0)`;
          spark.glow = s.glow * (0.35 + 0.65 * ((0.2126 * r + 0.7152 * g + 0.0722 * b) / 255));
        }
        bursts.push({
          x: clientX,
          y: clientY,
          start: performance.now(),
          sparks,
          settings: { ...s },
          canvas,
          ctx,
          ratio,
          left: box.left,
          top: box.top
        });
        while (bursts.length > MAX_BURSTS) remove(bursts[0]);
        if (!raf) raf = requestAnimationFrame(frame);
      };

      const onPointerDown = event => {
        if (event.pointerType !== 'mouse' || event.button !== 0) return;
        lastPress = { time: performance.now(), x: event.clientX, y: event.clientY };
        spawn(event.clientX, event.clientY);
      };

      const onClick = event => {
        if ('pointerType' in event && event.pointerType === 'mouse') return;
        if (event.detail === 0) {
          const target = event.target instanceof Element ? event.target : root;
          const rect = target.getBoundingClientRect();
          spawn(rect.left + rect.width / 2, rect.top + rect.height / 2);
          return;
        }
        const recent =
          performance.now() - lastPress.time < 700 &&
          Math.hypot(event.clientX - lastPress.x, event.clientY - lastPress.y) < 12;
        if (!recent) spawn(event.clientX, event.clientY);
      };

      root.addEventListener('pointerdown', onPointerDown);
      root.addEventListener('click', onClick);

      engineRef.current = {
        spark: (x, y) => {
          const rect = root.getBoundingClientRect();
          spawn(rect.left + (x ?? rect.width / 2), rect.top + (y ?? rect.height / 2));
        }
      };

      return () => {
        cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
        root.removeEventListener('pointerdown', onPointerDown);
        root.removeEventListener('click', onClick);
        for (const burst of [...bursts]) remove(burst);
        engineRef.current = null;
      };
    }, []);

    useImperativeHandle(
      ref,
      () => ({
        spark: (x, y) => engineRef.current?.spark(x, y)
      }),
      []
    );

    return (
      <div
        ref={rootRef}
        className={className}
        style={{ position: 'relative', width: '100%', height: '100%', isolation: 'isolate', ...style }}
      >
        {children}
        <span
          ref={layerRef}
          aria-hidden="true"
          style={{
            position: 'absolute',
            inset: 0,
            zIndex: layer === 'back' ? -1 : 1,
            overflow: 'visible',
            pointerEvents: 'none'
          }}
        />
      </div>
    );
  }
);

ClickSpark.displayName = 'ClickSpark';

export default ClickSpark;
```
